Furthermore, a user deployment study reveals that 93.3% of participants failed to detect the malicious logic, highlighting a severe blind spot in the security of modern AI supply chains.

</details>


### [137] [Implementing Homomorphic Encryption-Based Logic Locking in System-on-Chip Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28542)
*Ye Ziyang,Makoto Ikeda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于二元环学习带误差（Binary Ring-LWE）算法的逻辑锁定方案，并将其集成到RISC-V片上系统中，用于保护特权切换过程中的关键逻辑。该方案避免向用户直接暴露传统逻辑锁定所需的原始参数，但带来约6%至7%的硬件资源开销和约2.6微秒的解锁延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑锁定方法通常需要向使用方提供锁定参数，可能增加关键设计信息泄露的风险。研究旨在通过基于Ring-LWE的密码学机制保护处理器特权切换相关逻辑，在不公开敏感锁定参数的前提下实现安全解锁。

Method: 作者设计了一个基于二元Ring-LWE算法的锁定模块，并将其部署在RISC-V SoC的特权切换关键路径中。该模块通过密码学验证机制控制受保护逻辑的解锁，而非依赖直接分发的传统锁定密钥或参数。实验通过比较集成锁定模块前后的LUT、寄存器资源占用及解锁时间，评估其硬件成本与性能影响。

Result: 锁定模块自身使用3519个查找表（LUT）和2645个寄存器。与未加锁的基线系统相比，完整系统的LUT开销增加6.0%，寄存器开销增加6.9%。一次解锁过程约需2.6微秒。性能影响主要集中于涉及特权切换的系统级操作，而普通用户态计算效率基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 该方案证明了Ring-LWE密码学机制可用于RISC-V SoC逻辑锁定，并能在不暴露原始锁定参数的情况下保护关键控制逻辑。其资源开销处于中等水平，解锁延迟对用户态计算影响有限；但其实际适用性仍取决于目标系统对特权切换延迟、FPGA资源预算及密码学实现成本的容忍度。

Abstract: This study presents a logic locking scheme based on the binary Ring Learning With Errors algorithm, implemented in a RISC-V System-on-Chip design. Unlike traditional logic locking methods that require providing users with raw locking parameters, the proposed approach secures critical logic paths in the privilege switching process without exposing these sensitive parameters. The implemented locking module itself consumes 3519 Look-Up Tables and 2645 Registers, leading to an overall overhead of 6.0% in Look-Up Tables and 6.9% in Registers compared to the baseline system. The unlock process requires about 2.6 us, introducing moderate performance impact, primarily affecting system-level operations while preserving user-level computational efficiency.

</details>


### [138] [Benign on Label, Malicious by Design: Clean-Label Dormant-to-Activated Backdoor via Machine Unlearning with Removable Camouflage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27936)
*Dongdong Zhao,Can Li,Xiang Yao,Fan He,Qihang Ge,Baogang Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种洁净标签的“遗忘激活型后门”框架：后门在训练后保持低攻击成功率，仅在指定训练样本被机器遗忘后显著激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后门通常在植入后立即生效，容易在实际利用前暴露。现有遗忘激活方法难以同时满足遗忘前低攻击成功率、遗忘后高激活强度、洁净标签约束及现实删除请求等要求。

Method: 作者将问题表述为双层优化，并采用双生成器交替学习两类样本：其一为样本特异性触发器，用于建立持久的触发器到目标类别的潜在关联；其二为标签一致的伪装样本，用于形成可被遗忘移除的抑制作用。通过模拟潜在后门建立和机器遗忘过程，使删除少量伪装样本后解除抑制并激活后门。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10上，方法相较代表性后门基线在多种遗忘算法下表现出更低的遗忘前攻击成功率，以及更强的遗忘后激活效果。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在洁净标签和现实删除约束下，通过协调持久潜在关联与可移除抑制机制，可以实现较可靠的“休眠—激活”后门状态转换。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks often become effective immediately after backdoor implantation and may therefore be exposed before exploitation. Machine unlearning activated dormant backdoors mitigate such behavioral exposure by remaining inactive after training and becoming effective only after selected training records are unlearned. However, existing methods struggle to simultaneously achieve a low pre-unlearning attack success rate and strong post-unlearning activation under clean-label constraints and realistic unlearning requests. Achieving this transition requires jointly establishing a persistent latent association and a removable suppressive influence. To address this challenge, we propose a clean-label unlearning-activated backdoor framework based on dual-generator learning and formulate it as a bilevel optimization problem: By simulating latent backdoor establishment and machine unlearning, the framework alternately learns sample-specific triggers that establish a latent trigger-to-target association and label-consistent camouflage samples that provide removable suppression. Once a small subset of camouflage samples is unlearned, the suppression is lifted and the dormant backdoor is activated. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-10 show that our method maintains lower pre-unlearning attack success rates while achieving stronger post-unlearning activation across multiple unlearning algorithms than representative backdoor baselines. These results demonstrate that reliable dormancy-to-activation transitions can be achieved by coordinating a persistent latent association with removable suppression under clean-label and realistic deletion constraints.

</details>


### [139] [Safeguards Based on Copyable Context Cannot Provide Reliable Safety for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27951)
*Pingyu Wu,Lingyao Zhu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文讨论大语言模型安全机制在“双重用途”任务中的结构性局限：若模型只能依据用户可复制的请求与交互证据判断是否回答，就无法同时实现高能力可用性、可靠安全性与开放访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常在不了解答案实际下游用途的情况下决定是否响应。同一能力既可服务于合规专业人员，也可能被滥用；而恶意用户能够模仿正常请求、身份叙述和对话历史。因此，基于表面文本证据的安全策略存在不可避免的识别困难。

Method: 作者将模型输出的“能力释放”与系统可观察到的“下游用途证据”形式化区分。在证据可被复制的条件下，推导了保持有用回答时攻击者可获得帮助程度的精确最坏情形下界，并据此提出安全三难困境。随后分析可信凭证机制：通过引入难以复制、且能预测真实下游用途的信息，补充现有内容安全措施；同时给出彻底消除该下界所需的更强条件。论文还结合双重用途评测、自适应规避测试与已部署的可信访问项目检验其实践意义。

Result: 核心结果是“有用能力、可靠安全、开放访问”三者不能同时充分满足：在开放访问且判断依据可复制时，只要系统保留足够有用的能力，滥用者在最坏情况下也能获得不可低于某一水平的帮助。可信凭证可降低该风险，因为其提供了比文本声明更难伪造的用途相关信息；但仅有凭证并不必然完全消除风险，仍需满足更强的可验证性与用途预测条件。

Conclusion: 论文强调，单纯改进提示词过滤、分类器或对话审查无法从根本上解决双重用途模型的访问控制问题。要提高安全性，需要将能力供给与可验证的访问资格、用途证据及分层授权结合。其理论贡献在于明确了可复制证据条件下的安全下界，实践贡献在于为可信访问、凭证认证和差异化能力开放提供了设计依据。

Abstract: Large language model safeguards decide whether to answer before seeing how an answer will be used. This creates a basic problem for dual-use tasks: the same answer can help an authorized professional or an attacker, while an attacker can imitate a benign request and interaction history. We separate the capability released by the model from the evidence available about downstream use. When that evidence is copyable, we derive the exact worst-case floor on attacker assistance while preserving useful answers. The result yields a safety trilemma: Useful Capability, Reliable Safety, and Open Access cannot coexist. We then show how a trusted credential can complement existing safeguards by adding hard-to-copy information that predicts actual downstream use, and identify the stronger condition needed to eliminate the floor. Evidence from dual-use evaluations, adaptive attacks, and deployed trusted-access programs supports the practical relevance of these conditions.

</details>


### [140] [Driving up Inference Energy on SNNs: Per-Sample and Universal Sponge Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27990)
*Spyridon Raptis,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了原生事件驱动脉冲神经网络（SNN）面临的输入空间能效攻击风险：攻击者可在基本不改变分类结果的情况下，显著增加脉冲活动和突触计算量，从而提高边缘设备的推理能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN依赖稀疏二值脉冲通信，通常具有较低能耗，适用于持续运行的电池供电边缘设备。然而，传统以分类正确性为核心的监控机制难以发现仅增加计算负载、但不明显影响预测标签的攻击。已有研究主要针对率编码SNN中的逐样本攻击，原生事件输入及可复用攻击扰动的威胁尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 作者研究两类面向原生事件二值输入的海绵攻击。第一类为逐样本攻击：利用基于梯度的优化方法，为每个输入生成定制化对抗脉冲序列，以最大化突触操作数（SynOps）并约束预测类别保持不变。第二类为通用攻击：离线计算固定的二值扰动，并通过异或（XOR）应用于后续所有输入，无需针对单个样本重复优化。实验覆盖NMNIST、SHD和IBM DVS Gesture数据集上的三个SNN模型，并将SynOps增幅映射为Loihi-1硬件上的估计能耗。

Result: 逐样本攻击使三个数据集上的单次推理SynOps提高1.5至2.6倍，同时至少98%的测试样本保持原预测类别。通用攻击的效果较弱，但仍可使SynOps提高1.09至1.24倍，并且因其固定扰动可反复使用，具有更强的实际部署可行性。按照Loihi-1能耗估计，攻击带来的单次推理额外能耗范围为14微焦至13.24毫焦。

Conclusion: 原生事件驱动SNN不仅可能遭受影响预测结果的对抗扰动，也易受以增加能耗为目标的效率攻击。尤其是通用二值扰动能够在不进行逐输入优化的条件下持续提升推理负载，长期累积可能显著缩短持续运行边缘设备的电池寿命。论文表明，SNN安全评估和防护机制应同时监控预测行为、脉冲活动、SynOps及能耗异常。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spike events rather than dense activations, enabling energy-efficient inference on neuromorphic hardware and motivating their use in always-on, battery-powered edge systems. We show that this same efficiency advantage creates a distinct security risk: sponge attacks can increase inference-time spike activity and synaptic workload, inflating energy consumption while remaining difficult to detect through correctness-based monitoring alone. Prior input-space efficiency attacks on SNNs have focused on per-sample optimization, primarily in rate-coded settings. We extend this threat to native event-based binary inputs and study two attack models. First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [141] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出针对脉冲神经网络的洁净标签时间投毒方法：仅改变目标类别事件流的时间戳，不修改标签及每个像素、极性的事件总数，从而在时间聚合后保持样本表观一致，却诱导模型产生后门行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN后门研究主要依赖脏标签投毒，即将带触发器样本重标为攻击者指定类别；这类设定较易暴露。论文旨在研究更隐蔽的洁净标签威胁，并揭示将事件流沿时间轴聚合的检测与防御方法为何难以发现纯时间维度的异常。

Method: 作者对目标类别训练事件流施加固定时间戳变换，保持像素位置、事件极性及其计数不变，仅重排或调整事件的时序。实验覆盖3个神经形态数据集，并采用卷积式和Transformer式SNN受害模型。研究进一步通过投毒比例与触发器形状消融分析攻击条件；同时评估适配SNN的既有后门防御，并提出基于逐时间步事件质量分布的无模型检测器。

Result: 在最强实验配置中，该方法的攻击成功率达到1.00。时间轴被压缩后再检查的防御机制由于丢失时序信息而天然失效；特征空间检测方法仅能在部分配置下识别投毒样本。作者提出的逐步事件质量检测器能够检测所评估的时间变换，说明攻击虽能规避基于时间聚合的检测，但其隐蔽性仍存在可被利用的边界。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，SNN及事件数据中的后门风险不仅来自标签篡改或静态空间触发器，也可由纯粹的时间结构操纵造成。防御设计必须保留并分析完整时序，而不能仅依赖事件计数、帧化表示或时间聚合特征。论文将自身定位为首个在SNN和神经形态事件数据上系统评估的洁净标签后门研究。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [142] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“智能体编排蒸馏”（AHD）框架，研究攻击者仅通过黑盒交互提取基于大语言模型的自主多智能体系统推理时编排机制所带来的知识产权泄露风险，并提出一种兼顾系统效用的欺骗式防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时编排机制决定多智能体系统如何协调推理与行动，其构建通常需要大量工程投入和计算资源，具有较高知识产权价值。现有研究主要关注静态、预配置多智能体架构的泄露问题，尚未充分评估动态推理过程中涌现的编排行为是否也可被外部主体复现或窃取。

Method: 作者将“编排提取”形式化为新的安全问题，并构建风险评估框架。AHD包含两个阶段：预蒸馏阶段根据目标智能体的响应推断其推理时编排行为，并构建初始编排器；后蒸馏阶段则通过迭代优化初始编排器，使其行为模式进一步贴近目标智能体。论文还设计欺骗式防御，通过干扰提取过程来降低攻击成功率，同时尽量维持受保护智能体的正常效用。

Result: 在多个真实世界自主多智能体系统及不同底座大语言模型上的实验表明，AHD能够有效复现目标系统的推理时编排能力，说明该类系统存在显著的知识产权泄露风险。所提出的欺骗式防御能够降低编排提取的有效性，并在一定程度上保持原系统的任务能力。

Conclusion: 动态推理时编排并非天然难以复制；黑盒交互足以使攻击者逐步学习并逼近其关键行为模式。论文的主要贡献是提出编排提取威胁模型、给出两阶段蒸馏方法与评测框架，并验证防御可行性。其意义在于提示多智能体系统设计者应将推理时编排视为需专门保护的核心资产，而不仅关注模型参数或静态架构。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks. AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [143] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统研究面向持续语音交互多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入风险，提出攻击方法、基准测试集与防御机制，并报告了较高攻击成功率及超过90%的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体会持续接收环境音频，其中可能混入非用户可控的声音。现有研究对传统语音设备声学攻击关注较多，但对能够与用户语音并行叠加、进而影响智能体任务执行的音频指令注入威胁缺乏系统评估与有效防护。

Method: 作者提出指令增强与场景隐藏两类并发音频注入技术，使恶意指令以较低可感知性叠加于用户语音中；构建AudioAgentSecurity基准，覆盖8类真实任务场景和10种攻击模式，并测试11个先进智能体。防御方面提出级联音频解耦与验证（CADV），结合声源分离与跨模态一致性分析，以识别疑似注入指令。

Result: 在Gemini 3 Pro等11个智能体上的评测显示，所提攻击对Gemini 3 Pro的平均攻击成功率达到69.10%。CADV在多类攻击向量上的检测成功率超过90%，优于仅依赖提示词层面的防御方法。真实动态环境中的志愿者实验进一步表明，攻击具备较高隐蔽性和有效性，而CADV能够较稳定地缓解该风险。

Conclusion: 论文表明，持续音频输入是多模态智能体的重要安全边界，并发音频提示注入可在真实场景中显著影响智能体行为。基于声学源分离和跨模态一致性验证的防御路线具有较强潜力，但摘要未充分说明其在复杂混响、重叠多人语音、低信噪比条件下的性能代价、误报率与实时性，后续应重点评估这些部署因素。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [144] [Secure Aggregation for Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning on Clinical EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28191)
*Pouya Rajabi,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向临床脑电数据的隐私保护联邦学习框架，以基于掩码的安全聚合为核心，在不传输原始数据的前提下进一步隐藏单个客户端模型更新，并评估不同安全配置的开销与训练兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习虽避免共享原始临床脑电数据，但聚合服务器仍可能从个体模型更新中推断敏感信息。医疗跨机构协作还面临客户端掉线、恶意参与者、记录关联与结果可验证性等实际问题，因此需要兼顾隐私、安全鲁棒性和工程可部署性的聚合机制。

Method: 框架基于Flower实现，采用掩码式安全聚合，并结合图结构通信、阈值秘密共享、面向客户端掉线的鲁棒聚合、本地更新裁剪，以及可选的基于布隆过滤器的隐私保护记录链接初始化模块。针对不同威胁模型，设计半诚实、恶意防护和辅助公证节点等配置；在模拟的跨机构医疗环境中，使用TUH EEG衍生数据并改变客户端配置进行比较评估。

Result: 在论文声明的安全假设下，安全聚合变体能够使聚合服务器无法直接获知单个客户端更新。各安全变体仍可支持联邦模型训练，但恶意场景防护及轻量级一致性检查会增加计算量、通信量和单轮训练时长。半诚实配置在安全方案中开销最低；恶意防护和辅助公证配置则以更高成本换取更强的一致性、完整性与轻量验证能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对临床脑电跨机构训练，掩码式安全聚合能够在保持联邦训练可行性的同时增强更新隐私。系统设计呈现明确的安全—效率权衡：若主要关注低开销，可选半诚实配置；若需要抵抗更强攻击并提高聚合结果可信度，则应采用恶意防护或辅助公证机制。

Abstract: Federated learning enables multiple institutions to train shared models without exchanging raw clinical EEG data, but it does not fully prevent privacy leakage from individual model updates. This paper presents a privacy-preserving federated learning framework for clinical EEG data using masking-based secure aggregation as the core protection mechanism. The framework combines graph-based communication, threshold secret sharing, dropout-resilient aggregation, local update clipping, an optional Bloom filter-based privacy-preserving record-linkage initialization module, and auxiliary-notary-based verifiability. It supports both semi-honest and malicious aggregation settings and is implemented using the Flower federated learning framework. The secure-aggregation variants are evaluated in a simulated cross-silo healthcare setting using TUH EEG-derived data under different client configurations. Under the stated assumptions, the secure variants hide individual updates from the aggregation server. The results show that these variants remain compatible with federated model training, although malicious-setting safeguards and lightweight consistency-checking mechanisms introduce additional computation, communication, and round-duration overhead. The semi-honest variant provides the lowest overhead among the secure configurations, while malicious and auxiliary-notary variants offer stronger consistency, integrity, and lightweight verification support at higher cost.

</details>


### [145] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述具身人工智能中世界模型的安全问题，提出覆盖数据、表示学习、状态落地、预测规划、执行与长期适应的全生命周期安全框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通过压缩观测、预测动作后果和支持规划提升具身智能能力，但其预测链路一旦受数据、传感器、提示词或反馈污染，风险可能传导至实际执行。因此需要将安全分析从单一模型组件扩展到完整生命周期。

Method: 文章构建生命周期威胁分类法，将数据投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示词注入、轨迹操纵及供应链风险映射到世界状态、动力学模型、可供性估计和安全代价等关键安全属性；同时归纳安全失效评估协议与分层防御措施。

Result: 综述指出，传统攻击在世界模型场景中会表现为状态认知失真、未来预测偏差、规划轨迹失效及安全约束误判。世界模型既可作为运行时安全屏障，也可能因被篡改或过度信任而形成“预测性安全幻觉”。

Conclusion: 世界模型安全应采用端到端治理，包括数据与模型溯源、鲁棒状态落地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障。未来评估需关注攻击如何跨阶段传播，并以物理执行层面的安全后果作为核心指标。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [CircuitProver: Agentic Lean 4 Theorem Proving with Reusable Circuit Proof Library for Hardware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27259)
*Ziyi Yang,Wenji Fang,Chen Chen,Zhiyao Xie,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出CircuitProver：一个基于Lean 4的智能体硬件形式化验证框架，通过自动建模、交互式证明构造与证明知识积累，实现参数化硬件设计的可复用定理证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型检查通常针对单个设计实例输出通过或失败，证明过程依赖求解器内部启发式，难以复用；而交互式定理证明虽能产生显式证明工件，但硬件形式化需要大量人工完成建模、不变量发现和证明开发。因此，作者希望降低硬件定理证明的专家依赖，并将已有证明经验迁移到相关设计任务中。

Method: CircuitProver自动将参数化硬件代码及其自然语言规范转换为可执行的Lean 4模型；随后，智能体根据Lean反馈迭代生成并修正机器可检查证明。系统将证明轨迹、已验证定理和证明策略蒸馏为可复用知识库：前者用于引导后续智能体推理，后者作为引理支持跨任务形式化复用。论文还构建了首个面向智能体硬件定理证明的基准，覆盖多类参数化设计、规范、证明任务与评测指标。

Result: 在63个基准任务上，CircuitProver全部完成证明。相比之下，未使用该框架知识积累能力的基础智能体完成率为92.1%，且平均需要约两倍的证明轮次。消融实验表明，积累的证明知识可减少相关任务中的重复证明构造，使证明长度降低16.3%，验证时间降低23.2%。

Conclusion: CircuitProver表明，结合Lean 4反馈驱动证明、自动硬件建模和跨任务证明知识积累，可将硬件验证从孤立的实例级检查推进为可解释、可机器检查且可复用的参数化证明流程。其主要贡献还包括针对智能体硬件定理证明的专用评测基准；不过摘要中的结论主要基于63项任务，面对更大规模工业设计、复杂时序语义及自然语言规范歧义时的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Modern integrated circuits (ICs) are becoming increasingly complex, making functional verification a major bottleneck. The dominant hardware formal verification methodology, model checking, verifies each design instance separately and exposes only pass/fail results, so the reasoning behind a proof stays locked inside solver heuristics and is repeatedly reconstructed across related designs. Interactive theorem proving instead yields explicit, reusable proof artifacts, but applying it to hardware remains largely manual, demanding expert effort for formalization, invariant discovery, and proof development. In this paper, we present CircuitProver, an agentic Lean 4-based verification framework supporting proof-accumulation and parameterized verification. CircuitProver automatically translates parameterized hardware designs and their natural language specifications into executable Lean 4 models. It then iteratively constructs machine-checked proofs through Lean feedback to establish that the hardware code complies with the specification. The proving traces and verified theorems are distilled into reusable libraries, where proving strategies guide future agent reasoning and verified lemmas support formal proof reuse across related hardware verification tasks. We further introduce the first benchmark suite for evaluating agentic hardware theorem proving, covering diverse parameterized hardware designs, specifications, proof tasks, and evaluation metrics. Across 63 tasks, CircuitProver successfully proves all benchmarks, while a vanilla agent solves 92.1% of them and requires twice as many proof rounds on average. Ablation studies show that accumulated proof knowledge reduces redundant proof construction across related verification tasks, reducing proof length by 16.3% and verification time by 23.2%.

</details>


### [147] [From Lecture Notes to Lean: Formalizing a Textbook on Probability Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27298)
*Shuo Deng,Kenneth W. Shum*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该工作报告了对一本十四章测度论概率教材进行 Lean 形式化的持续项目，目标是在教材内容与 Mathlib 通用测度论接口之间建立可复用、可审查的机器验证基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型能够大量生成看似合理的数学论证，数学工作的重点将更多转向证明验证、清晰阐释与成果复用。传统教材中的隐含假设和非正式表述也需要通过形式化获得精确刻画，从而服务教学与可靠的 AI 辅助数学。

Method: 项目以 Lean 和 Mathlib 为基础，对教材从 Riemann--Stieltjes 积分、概率测度到鞅和极限定理等内容进行机器检验形式化。对于可直接匹配的结论复用 Mathlib 定理；对于教材表述与库中一般化抽象不一致的情形，构造可审查的接口引理以连接两者。

Result: 该项目产出教材的机器检查伴随版本，并扩展了可供后续概率论形式化使用的基础设施。形式化过程使定理陈述、假设条件和证明逻辑显式化，读者能够检查教材结论的精确逻辑内容。

Conclusion: 教材级形式化不仅能够提高数学证明的可验证性和可复用性，也能改善教学中的概念澄清，并为构建可靠的 AI 辅助数学所需的概率论形式基础提供重要支撑。

Abstract: As large language models become increasingly capable of generating mathematical arguments, mathematics is likely to face not a scarcity of proofs but an abundance of plausible ones. In such an environment, verification, exposition, and incorporation into reusable mathematical infrastructure become central tasks. We report on an ongoing Lean formalization of "Measure-Theoretic Probability: With Applications to Statistics, Finance, and Engineering", a fourteen-chapter upper-level undergraduate textbook covering topics from Riemann--Stieltjes integration to martingales and limit theorems.
  The project produces a machine-checked companion to the textbook and contributes reusable infrastructure for future formalizations involving probability theory. A Lean formalization provides computer-checked statements and proofs, makes hypotheses explicit, and allows readers to inspect the precise logical content of textbook results. A central challenge is to bridge textbook-facing statements with Mathlib's more general measure-theoretic interfaces. We reuse Mathlib results when possible and introduce reviewable interface lemmas when the textbook formulation and library abstraction differ. The project illustrates how formalized textbooks can support teaching, clarify mathematical assumptions, and help build the formal foundations needed for reliable AI-assisted mathematics.

</details>


### [148] [Extension Types for Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27387)
*Nicolai Kraus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文将路径类型、名称赋值扩展类型和受控展开等“部分由边界条件确定的类型”统一为二层类型论（2LTT）中的可定义构造，并证明该框架对HoTT保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩展类型通常依赖额外的面演算、形状层或专门语义模型，导致不同类型理论中的类似机制难以统一比较。作者希望在不增加公理或新模型构造的前提下，给出一个通用且语义上免费的基础框架。

Method: 作者在二层类型论中定义扩展类型，利用标准HoTT模型天然可解释2LTT以及2LTT对HoTT的保守性，验证扩展类型规则。进一步证明Riehl–Shulman扩展类型演算中的规则可严格成立，其相对函数外延性等原先假设的公理可由定义推导。随后以保守性为工具，建立适当形式的立方胶合与单价性之间的等价，并在Agda--two-level中自动形式化主要结果。

Result: 任何HoTT基础理论的模型都可自然获得扩展类型模型，真正需要额外指定的仅是哪些映射属于cofibration。论文证明了扩展类型演算的完整有效性，并给出立方胶合等价于单价性的结果，为研究立方类型理论相对于传统HoTT的保守性提供了方法路线。

Conclusion: 二层类型论可作为扩展类型的统一、保守且可形式化的元框架：它减少了对专门公理和模型构造的依赖，同时能够用于比较不同类型理论，并为立方类型理论与传统HoTT之间保守性问题的后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Extension types are a concept in dependent type theory that has appeared in various contexts. The idea is to have types whose terms are partially determined, e.g. via a strict boundary condition. Standard examples are path types of cubical type theories (paths with fixed endpoints), Riehl and Shulman's name-giving extension types (terms fixed on subshapes), as well as the controlled-unfolding mechanism of cooltt and Agda (terms that are fixed if a condition is met). In each case, the type theory is equipped with a (meta-theoretic) face calculus, or shape layer, that governs their rules, and comes with intended semantics.
  We unify all these occurrences in a single framework where no new axioms or model constructions are needed, namely two-level type theory. This step, too, is free (semantically): the standard models of HoTT are automatically models of 2LTT, and the theory is conservative over HoTT. Extension types are definable, and the definition validates Riehl and Shulman's entire extension-type calculus: the rules hold strictly, and the postulated axioms, such as relative function extensionality, become theorems. In this way, every model of the base theory (HoTT) gives rise to a model of the same theory with extension types; the only genuine assumptions are which maps count as cofibrations.
  Conservativity makes the framework a tool for comparing type theories. We prove that cubical gluing, in a suitable formulation, is equivalent to univalence. On this basis, we suggest an approach toward the conjecture that cubical type theories are conservative over book HoTT, one of the central open problems of homotopy type theory.
  All results of the main body of the paper are auto-formalized in Agda --two-level, in a development that combines HoTT-internal arguments with reasoning that is external to HoTT.

</details>


### [149] [Some Experiments with Twee-Style Goal-Directedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27442)
*Stephan Schulz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向饱和式一阶定理证明的子句选择策略：优先处理与待证目标共享项的子句，并以不同于Twee中等式定义转换的方法实现该思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 饱和式定理证明的效率高度依赖于下一待处理子句的选择。传统启发式可能产生大量与证明目标关联较弱的推理，因此需要利用目标导向信息，提高搜索聚焦性并降低无效推导。

Method: 作者将Twee已验证有效的“偏好与猜想共享项的子句”思想推广至完整一阶逻辑。不同于通过加入等式定义来重写问题，本文采用基于共享项的替代性实现，对子句与猜想之间的项重合关系进行利用，以影响子句处理优先级。

Result: 摘要表明，该替代实现取得了“非常有前景”的实验结果，说明基于共享项的目标导向子句选择能够有效改善一阶饱和证明中的搜索表现。摘要未给出具体基准集、成功率、运行时间或与基线方法的量化比较。

Conclusion: 共享项信息可作为一阶饱和定理证明中有效的子句选择信号。该工作的重要贡献在于将已有思路推广到完整一阶情形，并提供一种无需依赖等式定义转换的实现路径；其实际优势仍需结合全文实验设置与统计数据进一步评估。

Abstract: In saturation-based theorem proving, selecting the next clause for processing is a major concern. Twee has successfully applied the idea of preferring clauses that share terms with the conjecture by adding equational definitions to transform the problem. In this paper, we apply the idea to the full first-order case, and offer an alternative implementation based on shared terms that shows very promising results.

</details>


### [150] [Certified Sequential Sweep Without Unrolling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27498)
*Tobias Seufert,Christoph Scholl*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以重定时为预处理、基于IC3并结合仿真推测不变式的顺序等价验证方法，可验证经过重定时及任意强度时序重综合后的电路，并生成验证证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序等价检查工具难以可靠处理“重定时后再进行时序重综合”的优化流程。通用工具通常依赖内部重综合及多个模型检查器的复杂编排，导致验证过程容易出错，且难以获得可认证的证明结果。

Method: 该方法首先对待验证问题实施重定时预处理，以缓解状态对应关系的困难；随后利用仿真生成候选不变式，并通过IC3框架进行归纳验证和反例搜索。该流程面向重定时与后续强时序重综合共同造成的顺序结构变化，同时输出可检查的验证证书。

Result: 在一组开源电路的重定时与重综合实验中，该方法相较最新硬件模型检查竞赛获胜工具组合所代表的通用认证模型检查器表现出显著更高的效率。与ABC等成熟但非认证的等价检查方法相比，其性能仍具竞争力，并具有互补优势。

Conclusion: 将重定时预处理、仿真驱动的不变式猜测与IC3验证结合，可有效解决复杂时序优化后的顺序等价验证问题。该方案不仅提升验证效率，还能够提供证书，因此适合需要高可信认证的电路优化验证场景。

Abstract: Verifying retiming followed by additional sequential resynthesis steps remains challenging for existing tools, limiting aggressive optimizations. Commonly-used equivalence checking tools rely on internal resynthesis operations and error-prone or- chestration of different model checkers, which makes certification difficult or even infeasible. We present an IC3-based technique that uses retiming as a preprocessing step and uses simulation to generate suspected invariants. Our technique efficiently verifies sequential equivalence problems under retiming and arbitrarily strong sequential resynthesis, and has the additional feature of producing certificates all the same. Our results on a selection of retimed and resynthesized open circuit designs show that our rather simple approach vastly outperforms the whole portfolio of the winner of the latest Hardware Model Checking Competition as a representative of general-purpose certifying model checkers. Compared to non- certifying approaches, like the mature equivalence checker of ABC, we are still competitive with additional complementary strengths.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [OwlPath: Lossless Knowledge Compression for LLM Bug Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27249)
*Bo Zhang,Ren Pan,Huan Chen,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 OwlPath：在 CodeGraph 之上构建 OWL2 本体推理层，将源代码结构语义编码为可查询知识图谱，以更少上下文定位与缺陷相关的多跳代码依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大模型的软件工程代理受限于上下文窗口，传统文本检索难以直接解析继承链、传递调用关系和接口实现等多跳结构依赖，导致代理需通过反复试错定位相关代码，增加令牌消耗与运行时间。

Method: OwlPath 使用 tree-sitter 解析多语言仓库，并将语言特定语义统一编码到 OWL2 本体。系统包含两部分：其一，利用 SPARQL 属性路径执行传递闭包查询，一次性获取多跳结构关联符号；其二，预计算约 3KB 的 OWL Software Knowledge Map，概括模块树、核心 API 与问题相关符号，用于引导首次检索定位目标模块。

Result: 在 18 个 SWE-bench Pro 实例上，OwlPath 的严格应用率为 68.4%，略高于 CodeGraph 基线的 66.7%；令牌使用量降低 28.8%，运行时间降低 39.5%。在 67 个实例的离线检索中，召回率从 0.226 提升至 0.464，为 2.06 倍；命中率从 59.7% 提升至 88.1%。在 37 个结构检索问题上，召回率由 4.4% 提升至 28.8%，对传递调用者和接口相关任务的准确率为 69%至80%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将代码结构以 OWL2 本体进行无损知识压缩，并结合传递推理与紧凑仓库摘要，可显著改善软件工程代理的结构化代码检索效率和上下文利用率。其端到端修复成功率提升有限，但在检索质量、令牌成本和执行效率方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: LLM-based software engineering agents are constrained by limited context windows: roughly 100K tokens must store structurally relevant code  subsets to resolve bugs. Standard retrieval models treat code as plain text, forcing agents to resolve multi-hop dependencies including subclass  chains, transitive callers and interface implementations through slow trial and error. We tackle this limitation with lossless knowledge  compression, encoding source code into an OWL2 ontology to answer structural queries using minimal relevant code fragments.
  We present OwlPath, an OWL2 reasoning layer atop CodeGraph, a widely used code intelligence platform with 500K+ GitHub stars, offering a unified  CLI for structural code retrieval. Powered by tree-sitter parsing, OwlPath supports multi-language repositories (Python, JavaScript,  TypeScript, Go, etc.) and encodes language-specific semantics into a unified OWL2 ontology. It adopts two complementary modules. First, a  transitive-closure engine fetches all structurally linked symbols via single SPARQL property-path queries, capturing multi-hop relations missed  by string matching. Second, the OWL Software Knowledge Map (OWL-SKM) precomputes a compact 3KB summary with module trees, core APIs and  issue-related symbols, directing agents to target modules in the first query.
  Evaluated on 18 SWE-bench Pro instances, OwlPath obtains a 68.4% strict-apply rate versus 66.7% for the CodeGraph baseline, cutting token usage  by 28.8% and runtime by 39.5%. In offline retrieval tests over 67 instances, OwlPath improves recall 2.06 times (0.464 vs 0.226) and reaches  88.1% hit rate compared to CodeGraph's 59.7%. On a 37-question structural retrieval benchmark, recall rises from 4.4% to 28.8%, with 69-80%  accuracy on transitive caller and interface tasks.

</details>


### [152] [Do Context Files Help Coding Agents? A Two-Agent Ablation Study on Real Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27250)
*Prakhar Khatri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过受控消融实验发现，向AI编程代理注入持久化上下文文件并未显著提升任务正确率；主要瓶颈在于代理的实现与工程推理能力，而非仓库知识缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGENTS.md、CLAUDE.md等上下文文件被广泛用于指导AI编程代理，但其实际效果的既有证据相互矛盾。论文旨在系统检验上下文注入是否真正改善代码任务完成质量，并解释不同研究结论不一致的原因。

Method: 作者在两个前沿编程代理、三个代码仓库中的17个真实任务上进行上下文注入策略消融，共评估288次运行，并以黄金测试集判定正确性。研究使用等价性检验评估效果边界，进一步对失败案例进行失效模式归因，并通过操纵实验检验真实AGENTS.md能否将接近成功的结果转化为通过。最后比较不同代理上的任务难度排序。

Result: 上下文策略对两个代理的正确率均没有可测量的影响，等价性检验将可能差异限制在约10至15个百分点以内。失败案例主要源于功能设计、模式选择和精确连接等实现能力不足，而不是缺少上下文文件可提供的仓库知识。操纵实验同样表明，真实AGENTS.md无法使接近通过的案例转为成功。不同代理对任务难度的排序具有较高相关性，但临界难度区间存在代理特异性。

Conclusion: 持久化上下文文件不应被视为提升AI编程代理正确率的通用有效手段。后续改进应重点投入代理的实现规划、代码模式选择和端到端集成能力。既有研究的矛盾结果可能来自单一代理实验所选任务落在不同代理各自的信息量较高的难度区间；论文提供的代码、数据和分析有助于复现与进一步验证。

Abstract: Persistent context files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md) are standard practice for guiding AI coding agents, yet evidence for their effectiveness is contradictory. We present a controlled ablation of context-injection strategy across two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex), 17 real tasks from 3 repositories (15 shared + 2 Codex-only), and 288 evaluated runs with gold-test evaluation. Context strategy does not measurably move correctness on either agent (bounded to <=10-15pp via equivalence testing). A failure-mode triage reveals why: agents fail on implementation skill---feature design, pattern selection, exact wiring---not missing repository knowledge that a context file could supply; a manipulation probe confirms the real AGENTS.md never converts a near-miss to a pass on either agent. We further show that borderline task difficulty is agent-specific (Spearman rho=0.75), offering a candidate explanation for prior contradictions: single-agent studies draw tasks from different agents' informative bands. We release all code, data, and analysis.

</details>


### [153] [AgentS4D: Benchmarking Runtime Risks across the Execution Lifecycle of LLM-Based Workspace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27294)
*Jiajun Zhou,Zhaoxuan Ke,Jihang Ye,Xuanze Chen,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentS4D提出了一个面向LLM工作区代理的全生命周期运行时安全基准，通过风险来源、诱导策略、目标危害及执行证据检查点，对代理在多步骤、带状态任务中的安全性进行系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理安全评测虽开始关注可执行测试和轨迹验证，但通常缺少统一框架来描述风险从何处进入、如何诱导不安全行为、造成何种危害，以及执行过程中何处可获得证据。仅以任务是否完成作为安全指标，也可能掩盖已发生的风险行为。

Method: 研究构建了沙箱化基准AgentS4D，包含四维运行时安全框架：6类风险入口、6类诱导策略、9类目标危害，以及覆盖执行生命周期的7个证据检查点。基准共纳入328个风险注入案例，并评估4种代理执行框架与5种LLM后端组成的20种配置，累计完成6,560次运行。

Result: 6,560次运行中有4,461次（68.0%）触发预设不安全信号；其中4,344次（占全部运行的66.22%）虽然不安全但仍完成任务。不同框架与模型的组合表现出显著差异；同一诱导策略经由不同风险载体传递时会产生不同安全结果，同一目标危害在不同载体和策略下的响应也不一致。

Conclusion: 任务完成率不能替代运行时安全性。代理安全评估应针对完整的“执行框架—模型”配置，在多样化风险入口、载体和诱导条件下进行测试，并在整个执行过程中保留可审计证据，以避免单一风险形式造成的安全盲区。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based workspace agents execute stateful, multi-step workflows across heterogeneous resources, external tools, and persistent state. Their safety must therefore be assessed from actions, side effects, and state changes throughout execution. Although recent benchmarks have advanced executable safety testing and trajectory-aware verification, they rarely provide a unified account of where risks enter, how they elicit unsafe behavior, which harms they target, and where supporting evidence appears during execution. We introduce AgentS4D, a sandboxed benchmark for lifecycle-wide runtime safety evaluation. Its four-dimensional runtime-safety framework uses six risk-entry sources, six induction strategies, and nine target harms to guide case construction, while seven lifecycle checkpoints organize post-run evidence. AgentS4D contains 328 risk-injected cases. We evaluate all 20 combinations of four harnesses (Hermes, OpenClaw, Claude Code, and Codex) and five LLM backends (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, MiniMax-M3, and Qwen3.7-Plus) on these cases, yielding 6,560 runs. Overall, 4,461 runs (68.0%) trigger prespecified unsafe signals. Across the 20 configurations, the observed safety of an agent system varies with both its harness-LLM pairing and how risk is introduced. Agent systems exhibit markedly different safety behavior when the same induction strategy reaches them through different risk carriers. They also respond differently to the same target harm when it is realized through different carriers and strategies. Moreover, 4,344 runs (66.22% overall) are unsafe yet complete. Thus, task completion cannot establish runtime safety, and testing only one form of a risk can conceal important weaknesses. Evaluations should examine complete agent configurations across diverse risk conditions and retain evidence throughout execution.

</details>


### [154] [A Taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27302)
*Anastasia Mavridou,Hazel M. Taylor,Sandy Lozito,Louise A. Dennis,Michael Fisher,Marie Farrell*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了一个面向人机协作团队的需求分类体系，以解决现有需求分散、缺乏统一框架的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统正进入高安全性和高任务复杂度应用场景，人类与机器人需要共同完成复杂任务。然而，相关需求分布于学术文献、工业资料、标准和监管指南中，难以系统支持需求获取、组织与分析。

Method: 作者从14个跨领域来源中提取361条需求，经过迭代式分类与修订，构建两级层次化分类法；随后邀请5名领域专家进行评估，并使用来自19个来源、覆盖6类人机协作领域的448条独立需求语料开展实用性验证。

Result: 分类法包含6个高层类别和21个低层子类别，覆盖信息提供、关系控制、决策支持、安全机制、性能监控及基础系统能力等需求维度。专家评估与独立语料验证表明，该体系具有一定的合理性和跨领域适用价值。

Conclusion: 该研究为人机团队协作需求提供了统一、结构化的描述框架，可用于整合分散需求来源，并支持自主系统的人机协作设计、需求工程、合规分析与后续评估。

Abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in safety- and mission-critical domains where humans and robots must operate as a team to complete complex tasks. Existing requirements for Human-Robot teamwork remain fragmented across disparate sources, with no unified framework that addresses complexities of collaborative Human-Robot tasks. We address this gap by presenting a taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork (HRT) requirements derived from analysis of (academic and industrial) literature, standards and regulatory guidance. We extracted a construction corpus of 361 requirements from 14 cross-domain sources. Through iterative classification and refinement, we develop a two-level hierarchical taxonomy comprising 6 high-level categories and 21 low-level subcategories that distinguish information provision, relational control, decision support, safety mechanisms, performance monitoring, and foundational system capabilities. We validate the taxonomy through expert evaluation with 5 domain specialists and a utility demonstration on an independently assembled corpus of 448 requirements drawn from 19 sources spanning six HRT domains.

</details>


### [155] [SIGIL: Compiling Agent Skills into Typed Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27309)
*Jayanaka Dantanarayana,Savini Kashmira,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“技能编译”（Skill Compilation）框架 SIGIL，将自然语言编写的智能体技能编译为可执行 harness，以提升过程遵循度并降低 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于散文式流程文件的技能需要模型在每次执行时重新推导控制流，容易跳过强制验证步骤；实验显示，尽管产物可通过输出检查，原始散文智能体仅完成其技能规定步骤的 56%。手工编写 harness 虽能解决该问题，但成本高且牺牲了自然语言技能易于创作的优势。

Method: SIGIL 将散文技能编译为 executable harness。其核心为 AG-IR，一种带类型的智能体中间表示，将模型负责的认知任务与代码负责的机制性流程分离：模型承担需要推理、生成或判断的部分，程序结构负责步骤编排、控制流和验证约束，从而把流程要求编码进运行时。

Result: 在涵盖 30 个技能、两代模型的评估中，编译后的 harness 完成 86% 的规定步骤，高于散文智能体的 56%；完整执行整个流程的频率提高至原来的 2.3 倍；token 消耗降至原来的 0.58 倍。更重要的是，harness 在两代模型上均维持 86% 的步骤完成率，而散文方案在不同模型间由 56% 波动至 68%。

Conclusion: SIGIL 表明，可将易于编写的自然语言技能自动转化为具有程序化控制结构的执行 harness，在不依赖特定模型能力的前提下，显著增强智能体对规定流程的遵循性、稳定性和执行效率。

Abstract: AI-Integrated agents increasingly acquire capability from skills: prose procedure files loaded into a model's context and run by a tool-calling loop. A skill is described to the runtime but never encoded in it, so the model re-derives its control flow on every run and may skip mandated verification. Across 30 skills and two model generations, a prose agent performs only 56% of the steps its own skill mandates, while producing artifacts that pass output checks. The remedy is known: write a harness, in which the procedure is program structure. However, hand-writing harnesses is tedious and discards the authoring surface that made skills succeed. To address this limitation, we introduce Skill Compilation, realized in SIGIL, which compiles a prose skill into an executable harness. At its center is AG-IR, a typed agentic intermediate representation separating model-owned cognition from code-owned mechanism. Compiled harnesses perform 86% of mandated steps, complete the full procedure 2.3x as often, and require 0.58x the tokens. Notably, the guarantee is model-independent: the harness holds at 86% across two model generations while prose swings from 56% to 68%.

</details>


### [156] [TrustChain-Review: A Risk-Adaptive Blockchain and Game-Theoretic Framework for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27310)
*Mohammad Naserameri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出TrustChain-Review框架，将区块链可验证证据、开发者—审查者—平台三方博弈及风险自适应治理结合，用于提升AI辅助软件开发中的代码审查可信度。完整证据治理在信誉与检测效果上最佳，但成本最高；风险自适应治理则以较低成本取得更优成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发提高了编码和审查效率，但也削弱了责任可追溯性：开发者可能提交验证不足的代码，审查者可能进行表面审查，集中式信誉记录也可能缺乏可审计性。因此，需要一种既能提供可信证据与激励约束，又能根据变更风险控制治理成本的代码审查机制。

Method: 框架由三部分构成：第一，基于区块链的证据层，用于记录和验证审查相关行为；第二，开发者、审查者与平台参与的三方博弈模型，用于刻画策略激励；第三，风险敏感治理规则，在预期收益超过治理成本时增强控制强度。实验采用经Diff Quality Estimation数据集校准的受控仿真，对六种治理配置进行30次独立运行，并评估信誉准确性、信任收敛、恶意审查检测、表面审查检测、平台净效用、治理成本和成本效率。

Result: 完整证据配置在信誉准确性、信任收敛以及恶意和表面审查检测方面表现最强，但治理成本也最高。风险自适应配置通过仅在必要时施加更严格控制，降低了治理成本并提高成本效率；不过，其信任与审查检测指标仍低于完整证据配置。

Conclusion: 对于高风险、强审计或影响范围较大的代码变更，应采用强证据驱动的完整治理，以优先保证可追溯性和审查可信度。对于常规或低风险贡献，风险自适应的选择性治理能在可信性与运营成本之间提供更可行的平衡。

Abstract: Context: AI-assisted software development can speed up coding and review, but it also makes accountability harder to establish. Developers may submit insufficiently verified code, reviewers may approve changes with limited inspection, and centralized reputation records may be difficult to audit. Objectives: This study introduces TrustChain-Review, a framework that combines verifiable evidence, strategic incentives, and risk-sensitive governance to support more trustworthy code review. Methods: The framework includes a blockchain-based evidence layer, a three-player game-theoretic model for developers, reviewers, and the platform, and a rule that applies stronger governance when the expected benefit justifies its cost. The evaluation uses a controlled simulation calibrated with the Diff Quality Estimation dataset. Six governance configurations are compared over 30 independent runs using reputation accuracy, trust convergence, malicious-review detection, superficial-review detection, net platform utility, governance cost, and cost-efficiency. Results: The full-evidence configuration produces the strongest reputation, trust, and detection results, but it also has the highest governance cost. The risk-adaptive configuration lowers this cost and improves cost-efficiency by applying stronger controls selectively, although its trust and detection results are lower than those of the full-evidence setting. Conclusion: Strong evidence-based governance is most appropriate for high-risk or audit-sensitive changes. For routine or lower-risk contributions, selective governance offers a more practical balance between trustworthiness and operational cost.

</details>


### [157] [PROGRESS: Property-Guided Regression Search for Semantic Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27359)
*Davis Tocheuk Mo,Noshin Ulfat,Matthew B. Dwyer,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PROGRESS 将意图驱动的性质测试与覆盖率引导的进化式回归测试生成结合，在保持深层程序状态探索能力的同时，利用独立语义预言机发现当前实现中的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的回归测试生成能够获得较高结构覆盖率，但其断言通常由被测系统行为派生，可能将既有缺陷固化为预期结果；独立的性质测试虽可表达语义约束，却缺乏到达深层状态及满足严格前置条件的有效搜索引导。

Method: PROGRESS包含三项设计：第一，从代码、文档及调用者/被调用者上下文提取意图信息，并通过语言模型生成可执行的 jqwik 性质，同时降低实现细节泄漏；第二，在 EvoSuite 的 DynaMOSA 中为每个性质加入搜索目标和性质感知适应度函数，奖励前置条件满足进展并优先选择可证伪性质的执行；第三，将性质参数与 jqwik 生成器绑定至不断演化的测试序列，使输入量化、覆盖率优化和缺陷发现能够协同进行。

Result: 在25个大型Java系统上的评估显示，PROGRESS在562个当前版本缺陷中检出328个，检出率为58%，而常规回归测试生成未检出缺陷；对于150个难以到达的性质，PROGRESS使其中70个满足全部前置条件，独立jqwik仅完成18个。消融实验进一步表明，文档以及调用者/被调用者上下文对生成有效、可执行的性质至关重要。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将高层意图性质转化为进化测试搜索目标，可以弥补回归断言缺乏独立语义预言机、性质测试缺乏状态可达性引导的双重不足。PROGRESS在结构探索能力基本保留的前提下显著提升了语义缺陷暴露能力，其核心贡献是性质生成、前置条件导向搜索和参数化输入绑定的端到端集成。

Abstract: Search-based regression-test generation effectively explores complex program structures, yielding high structural coverage, but its oracles are derived from the system under test: faults already present are recorded as expected behavior rather than exposed. Property-based testing offers independent semantic oracles, but depends on high-quality properties and gives little guidance for reaching deep states or satisfying selective preconditions.
  We present PROGRESS (PROperty-Guided REgression Search for Semantic Falsification), integrating intent-driven properties into coverage-guided, search-based evolutionary test generation to reach deep program states and detect violations of intended behavior. PROGRESS (1) extracts intent-bearing code context and uses a language-model pipeline to generate executable jqwik properties while limiting implementation leakage; (2) extends EvoSuite's DynaMOSA with a search objective and property-aware fitness function per property, rewarding progress through preconditions and prioritizing falsifying executions; and (3) binds property parameters and uses jqwik-provided generators to connect quantified inputs to evolving test sequences, steering generation toward coverage and bug-detection goals.
  We evaluate PROGRESS on 25 large-scale Java systems against regression-test generation, standalone property-based testing, and context ablations. PROGRESS detects 328/562 current-version bugs (58%) versus none for regression-test generation, and satisfies all preconditions for 70/150 hard-to-reach properties versus 18 for standalone jqwik. Ablations show documentation and caller/callee context are key to generating valid executable properties. PROGRESS preserves structural exploration while exposing faults missed by regression-derived assertions; we release a comprehensive artifact package.

</details>


### [158] [From Backlog Items to Security Guidance: Towards Continuous Security Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27374)
*Ignacio García Núñez,Florian Angermeir,Fabiola Moyón Constante*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一个基于自然语言处理的待办事项（backlog）增强系统，用于识别与安全相关的软件需求条目，并从安全需求文档中检索和生成对应指导，以支持受监管领域中的持续安全合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续软件工程中，待办事项通常仅含简短、自由文本描述，安全需求未被明确记录。工程师需要自行判断条目是否涉及安全，并查找适用的安全规范，导致需求识别滞后且一致性不足。

Method: 方法由两部分构成：其一，训练面向高召回率的安全相关性分类器，判断待办事项是否需要安全关注；其二，构建以安全需求文档为知识来源的四阶段检索增强生成（RAG）流程，为相关条目关联具体安全条款。研究发布了包含288个待办事项的数据集，由9名安全从业者标注，并在企业内部安全政策及CIS Benchmarks上开展工业场景初步评估。

Result: 标注数据的Fleiss κ为0.787，表明标注者之间具有较高一致性。分类器在分布内测试中取得F2=0.774；在5个既有基准上的零样本测试中，平均G-measure约为0.65，达到或超过多数已发表的传统机器学习与开源GPT基线。RAG流程共检索24项条款，其中12项被两名从业者评为至少4/5的相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究初步证明，NLP驱动的待办事项增强可在开发早期帮助工程师发现安全相关需求并获得文档依据。其主要价值在于将安全需求识别和条款关联前移至持续开发流程；但RAG结果的高相关条款比例仅约一半，且实践评估规模较小，仍需在更多项目、更多标注者及端到端工程效益指标上进一步验证。

Abstract: Continuous software engineering in regulated domains requires engineering teams to address security throughout the development lifecycle. Yet making security requirements explicit in backlog items is still problematic. Engineers must instead infer security relevance of backlog items from brief, free-form descriptions and often lack timely guidance on applicable requirements. We present an NLP-based backlog enrichment system that detects security-relevant backlog items and links them to relevant security requirements. The approach combines a security-relevance classifier with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline over security requirements documents. The approach was developed and evaluated in the context of a large enterprise in highly regulated domains. We present three contributions. First, we release a dataset of 288 backlog items labeled for security relevance by nine security practitioners, with substantial agreement (Fleiss' $κ=0.787$). Second, a recall-oriented classifier achieving $F2=0.774$ in-distribution and mean zero-shot G-measure $\approx 0.65$ across five established benchmarks, matching or outperforming most published classical-ML and open-source GPT baselines. Third, we preliminarily evaluated a four-stage security requirements document-grounded RAG pipeline with two practitioners on industrial backlogs using company-internal security policies and CIS Benchmarks. Of the retrieved 24 clauses, 12 were rated at least 4/5 for relevance. Our findings provide first indicators that NLP-based product backlog enrichment can support engineers in identifying security requirements early in the development process. With this work we aim to facilitate continuous security compliance through proactive introduction of security requirements in continuous software engineering.

</details>


### [159] [SWE-NFI: Studying and Benchmarking Coding Agents for Non-Functional Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27409)
*Pengyu Xue,He Yang Yuan,Xin Wang,Junkai Chen,Haonan Zhang,Boyuan Chen,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SWE-NFI基准，用于评估代码智能体在不改变可观察行为前提下进行非功能性改进的能力。基准包含188个来自开源Python项目真实合并拉取请求的任务，并以92条可执行规则结合功能测试进行评测。实验显示，现有智能体的功能正确性较高，但非功能性改进能力仍明显落后于人类开发者，尤其在代码结构改进方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体评测主要关注功能正确性，例如生成的补丁是否修复缺陷或通过测试；但实际软件开发中，开发者还会持续进行重构、可读性提升、维护性优化等不改变外部行为的改进。由于缺乏系统化基准和可执行评价标准，智能体在此类非功能性改进任务上的能力尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 作者构建SWE-NFI基准，从真实开源Python项目中已合并的拉取请求提取188项任务。论文将面向开发者的非功能性改进形式化为92条可执行规则，并设计联合评测套件：一方面通过测试验证补丁保持功能正确性，另一方面使用规则检查补丁是否实现目标非功能性改进。随后，作者在该基准上评测多种先进商业及开源代码智能体，并与人工参考实现进行比较。

Result: 表现最好的智能体达到70.0%的功能正确性率，说明当前系统能够在相当一部分任务上维持或恢复预期功能。然而，所有被评测智能体在整体非功能性改进能力上普遍弱于人类开发者。差距在结构性代码改进中尤为突出：智能体的非功能性改进得分仅为0.0至1.3，而人工参考得分为1.5。

Conclusion: SWE-NFI补充了仅评估功能正确性的代码智能体基准体系，为评估行为保持型软件质量改进提供了可复现的任务集、规则体系和测试流程。研究表明，功能正确并不等同于高质量的软件工程修改；未来代码智能体需要进一步提升重构、结构优化与可维护性改进等能力。

Abstract: Although coding agents have achieved impressive performance on correctness-oriented benchmarks, their ability to make behavior-preserving non-functional improvements (NFIs) remains underexplored. In real-world software development, developers continuously improve software quality without changing observable behavior, yet existing benchmarks primarily evaluate functional correctness and provide limited support for assessing these non-functional improvements.
  In this paper, we present SWE-NFI, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on NFIs beyond functional correctness. Our benchmark contains 188 tasks constructed from real merged pull requests in open-source Python projects. We operationalize developer-oriented NFIs into 92 executable rules and develop a comprehensive evaluation suite that combines functional correctness testing with rule-based NFI evaluation.
  We evaluate state-of-the-art commercial and open-source coding agents. Although the best-performing agent achieves a 70.0\% functional correctness rate, all evaluated agents generally fall short of human developers in overall NFI capability. The gap is particularly evident for structural code improvements, where agents' NFI scores range from 0.0 to 1.3, compared with 1.5 for the human reference. Our benchmark and findings provide a reproducible foundation for evaluating and advancing coding agents beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [160] [LimICE: Integrating LLM into ICE Framework for Efficient Loop Invariant Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27606)
*Kai Fan,ShiWen Yu,GuangSheng Fan,HaoAng Chi,WanWei Liu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出增量式循环不变式合成框架 Incremental ICE，并实现工具 LimICE。该方法将 IC3 的增量思想融入 ICE 学习框架，通过大语言模型生成有序引理序列、反例过滤和 ICE-DT 兜底，显著提升了线性与非线性基准上的求解数量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的循环不变式生成方法通常采用单体式学习，即直接学习一个完整不变式公式。由于学习过程难以被严格约束，这类方法在复杂任务中往往无法同时满足初始性、归纳性和安全性等要求。论文观察到，循环不变式更适合被视为按顺序逐步构造的引理序列，因此需要一种支持增量合成与逐步验证的学习框架。

Method: 作者提出 Incremental ICE，将 IC3 的逐层、增量式证明思想整合到 ICE 不变式学习框架中。其核心包括：为每个引理定义专门的学习目标；设计反例过滤机制，以保证逐步学习过程的可靠性；利用大语言模型生成有序引理序列；在大语言模型生成的引理不足时，使用决策树方法 ICE-DT 作为回退机制补全不变式。基于该框架，作者实现了 LimICE 工具。

Result: 在367个线性基准上，LimICE 成功求解349个，平均耗时15.2秒；在50个非线性基准上，成功求解47个，平均耗时8.8秒。相较于最先进的大语言模型基线，LimICE 在不同基准上多解决12%至24%的实例，并快36%至63%。相较于强非大语言模型基线，LimICE 在线性和非线性基准上分别至少多解决86个和27个实例。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将循环不变式建模为可验证的有序引理序列，并采用增量式学习与反例过滤，能够缓解单体式学习难以生成完整不变式的问题。LimICE 的实验结果显示，该框架在求解能力和运行效率上均具有明显优势；不过，摘要未说明引理生成失败模式、不同大语言模型的敏感性以及更大规模或更复杂程序上的泛化情况。

Abstract: Loop invariant synthesis is a fundamental problem in program verification, yet the inherent undecidability makes it highly challenging. Recent studies have increasingly employed various machine learning techniques to generate loop invariants. However, most of these methods adopt a monolithic approach. Due to the inability to strictly constrain the learning process, learning-based methods struggle to simultaneously consider all necessary conditions and generate complete invariants when tackling complex problems. In fact, a loop invariant is often an ordered sequence of lemmas, rather than a single invariant formula. This motivates us to propose Incremental ICE, a novel learning framework for incremental synthesis. Our framework integrates the incremental philosophy of IC3 into the general invariant learning framework ICE. By defining a lemma-specific learning objective and introducing a counterexample filtering mechanism, we can achieve sound incremental learning. Under this framework, we instantiate a loop invariant synthesis tool, LimICE, which leverages LLMs to generate the ordered sequence of lemmas and incorporates ICE-DT as a fallback mechanism to complement the lemma sequence. Experiments on 367 linear benchmarks and 50 nonlinear benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. LimICE solves 349 (out of 367) linear problems on an average of 15.2 seconds and 47 (out of 50) nonlinear problems on an average of 8.8 seconds. Compared to the state-of-the-art LLM-based baseline, our approach solves 12-24% more instances while running 36-63% faster across linear and nonlinear benchmarks. LimICE also consistently outperforms strong non-LLM baselines and solves at least 86 and 27 additional instances on the linear and nonlinear benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [161] [Not as Sweet by Another Name: An Empirical Study of Format Robustness in LLM Document Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27648)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Xianyun Cheng,Tianlin Li,Yuwei Zheng,Yue Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向大语言模型文档工作流的格式感知变形测试框架，发现相同语义内容仅因文档格式变化就可能显著降低系统准确率并改变决策结果；用户侧轻量缓解措施可在无需重新训练模型的条件下部分恢复稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型测试主要关注单一文本提示词的鲁棒性，而实际应用 increasingly 采用文件上传和文档处理流程。同一内容可通过CSV等不同格式输入，但格式差异是否会影响端到端工作流的行为与可靠性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 研究构建了一个格式感知的变形测试框架，定义三类变形关系，用于比较同一语义内容在不同文档格式下的工作流输出一致性。实验覆盖4类代表性大语言模型工作流、4项真实任务和4种文档格式，共执行48,000次工作流测试；此外设计了无需模型再训练的用户侧轻量化缓解策略。

Result: 实验表明，文档格式变化会带来系统性可靠性风险：仅切换输入格式，准确率最高可下降53.63%，超过41%的样例出现决策漂移。所提出的缓解策略最多可恢复44.21%的格式诱发决策漂移，说明部分问题能够通过输入或使用流程层面的改进得到缓解。

Conclusion: 文档格式并非对语义内容完全中性的外层封装，而是影响大语言模型文档工作流行为、准确性和决策一致性的关键因素。论文建议在实际部署中引入格式鲁棒性测试、跨格式一致性校验及相应防护机制。

Abstract: LLM-driven software systems are rapidly evolving from plain-text conversations to document-centric end-to-end workflows, where the same semantic content can be delivered in diverse document formats (e.g., CSV) through file upload interfaces. Yet existing testing work focuses on the robustness and reliability of models and systems whose input is a single prompt string, leaving a critical question unanswered: Can these document workflows maintain robust behaviors when the same content arrives in a different document format? To fill the gap, in this paper, we propose a format-aware metamorphic testing framework with three metamorphic relations to comprehensively evaluate the format robustness of end-to-end LLM document workflows. Based on this framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study spanning four representative LLM workflows, four real-world tasks, and four document formats, comprising a total of 48,000 workflow executions. Our findings reveal that format variation poses a systematic and serious threat. Merely switching formats can cause accuracy to drop by up to 53.63% and trigger decision drifts in over 41% of instances. We further design lightweight mitigation strategies from the users' perspective that recover up to 44.21% of format-induced decision drift without model retraining. Our study demonstrates that document format is not a neutral wrapper but a critical factor affecting the reliability of LLM software systems, calling for corresponding testing and safeguards in the deployment in real-world high-stakes scenarios.

</details>


### [162] [Can Large Language Models Resolve Real Java Merge Conflicts? An Evaluation with a Calibrated LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27674)
*Bowen Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究真实Java合并冲突中的LLM自动解决与可扩展评测。经人工标签校准的LLM评审器表明，LLM在覆盖率公平比较下的开发者方案匹配率为55%—59%，高于传统工具AutoMerge的36.7%；优势主要来自LLM几乎不弃权，而非单次可处理冲突上的准确率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构化或半结构化合并工具在规则、启发式不适用时通常直接弃权，导致大量冲突仍需人工处理。LLM能够为几乎所有冲突生成候选解，但逐一依赖人工判断其质量难以扩展。因此，研究同时关注：如何构建可自动尝试解决冲突的LLM代理，以及如何以可信、规模化的方式评价其结果。

Method: 作者基于ConflictBench中的真实Java合并冲突，构建“生成—验证—重试”式LLM求解代理。代理仅使用推理时信号，包括冲突标记、Java解析器和重复声明检查，不访问开发者真实解决方案。评测采用双指标：其一是基于G-Eval的“开发者方案匹配”LLM评审器，并在使用前以ConflictBench人工标签进行校准；其二是完全确定性的结构有效性检查，不依赖LLM。评审器在292个有人类标注的样本上进行元评测。

Result: 校准后的LLM评审器达到100%精确率，即没有错误接受，但召回率为64.6%；因此，其接受结果可信，而报告的匹配率应视为保守下界。按该评审器，LLM求解器在真实冲突中约55%能匹配开发者方案。在覆盖率公平的比较中，LLM表现为55%—59%，AutoMerge为36.7%，LLM领先约18—22个百分点。该差距几乎完全源于覆盖率：传统工具会对20%—90%的冲突弃权，而强制生成解决方案的LLM不弃权。另有5个结果未通过确定性结构检查，其中4个却被LLM评审器接受，表明结构正确性不能交由LLM评审器单独判断。

Conclusion: LLM可显著提升自动合并冲突解决的总体覆盖能力，并在真实Java冲突上取得优于传统工具的保守匹配下界。但其优势主要是减少弃权，而不是证明其在同等可处理样本上更准确。论文还说明，LLM作为评审器必须先经人工标签校准，且应与确定性语法和结构验证结合；尤其不能用LLM判断替代结构正确性检查。

Abstract: Merge conflicts are a recurring cost of collaborative software development, and the traditional structured and semi-structured merge tools that address them frequently abstain: when their heuristics do not apply, they leave the conflict unresolved. Large language models (LLMs) can instead produce a candidate resolution for almost any conflict, but measuring whether those resolutions are actually good at scale is hard, because obtaining human desirability judgments for every model output does not scale. We study both problems together on real Java merge conflicts from ConflictBench. We first build an LLM solver as a generate-validate-retry agent that uses only inference-time signals (conflict markers, a Java parser, and duplicate-declaration checks) and never sees the developer's answer. We then evaluate its resolutions with a two-metric suite: (1) a developer-match LLM-as-judge implemented as a G-Eval metric and, crucially, calibrated against ConflictBench's human labels before use, and (2) a deterministic structural-validity check that uses no LLM. On a meta-evaluation of 292 human-labeled cases, the judge reaches 100% precision (zero false accepts) at 64.6% recall, so every acceptance is trustworthy and every downstream rate is a conservative lower bound. Under this validated judge, LLM solvers match the developer's own resolution on about 55% of true conflicts (conservative floor), and under a coverage-fair comparison the LLMs (55-59%) beat the strongest traditional tool (AutoMerge, 36.7%) by roughly 18-22 points; the edge comes almost entirely from coverage, not raw accuracy, since the tools abstain on 20-90% of conflicts while the LLM under forced resolution abstains on none. Finally, the LLM judge accepted 4 of the 5 resolutions that fail the deterministic structural check, evidence that structural correctness must not be delegated to an LLM.

</details>


### [163] [A comparative analysis of automated techniques for security bug report identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27893)
*Muhammad Laiq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究系统比较多种自动化方法识别安全相关缺陷报告，发现SetFit总体最佳，RoBERTa表现接近，传统机器学习在部分场景仍具竞争力，而GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本条件下效果较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全相关缺陷报告的及时识别有助于缩短漏洞暴露窗口，但人工筛选成本高、易出错且难以扩展。现有研究的模型、基线和实验设置不一致，导致不同方法难以公平比较，实践中缺乏明确的选型依据。

Method: 作者在基准数据集上统一评估Logistic Regression、Support Vector Machines、Random Forest、GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa与SetFit。除项目内实验外，还开展跨项目实验，以考察迁移学习对数据有限项目的帮助及其潜在风险。

Result: SetFit取得最高总体性能，F1值为0.80，并在4个数据集中的3个上优于其他方法。RoBERTa具有较强竞争力，在部分项目中接近SetFit；传统方法，尤其逻辑回归，在特定场景下仍是可靠基线。GPT-5.2无论零样本还是少样本设置均表现不佳。跨项目迁移可提升训练数据较少项目的效果，但对项目特征显著的场景可能造成性能下降。

Conclusion: 对于安全相关缺陷报告分类，SetFit是当前比较中最值得优先考虑的方法，RoBERTa可作为性能接近的替代方案，逻辑回归应保留为低成本强基线。模型选择不应只看总体分数，还需结合项目数据规模、领域特异性及是否需要跨项目迁移；大语言模型在该任务上的直接零样本或少样本应用并不具备优势。

Abstract: Timely identification of security-related bug reports is essential to minimize the window of vulnerabilities in software systems. Manually screening incoming bug reports to identify security-related issues is time-consuming, error-prone, and non-scalable for large-scale software systems. Thus, a variety of automatic techniques, including traditional machine learning (ML) techniques and large language models, have been proposed to facilitate this task. However, the literature remains fragmented. Most studies introduce or optimize a particular technique and evaluate it against a limited set of baselines, often under different experimental setups. As a result, it is difficult to compare their results and draw reliable conclusions about the effectiveness of existing approaches, leaving researchers and practitioners without clear guidance on which techniques are most suitable for the task.
  To address this gap, we conducted a comparative analysis of several promising automated techniques to identify security-related bug reports using benchmark datasets. We evaluated Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines, Random Forest, OpenAI's GPT-5.2, BERT-base, RoBERTa, and SetFit (a state-of-the-art few-shot learning framework).
  Our results indicate that SetFit achieves the best overall performance, achieving an F1-score of 0.80 and outperforming other techniques on three of the four datasets. RoBERTa performs competitively and approaches SetFit in some projects, while traditional ML techniques, particularly Logistic Regression, remain a strong baseline in certain contexts. In contrast, GPT-5.2 performs poorly in both zero-shot and few-shot settings. In addition, cross-project experiments demonstrate that transfer learning can improve performance for projects with limited data, but may degrade results for projects with strong project-specific characteristics.

</details>


### [164] [The Case for Vibe Modeling: A Missing Step in AI-Based Trustworthy Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27923)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Michael Mittermaier,Judith Michael*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“氛围建模（vibe modeling）”作为自然语言交互与代码生成之间的轻量级中间表示，以改善大语言模型辅助软件开发中的意图保留、可理解性、验证性、可追溯性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的软件开发实践过度聚焦于直接生成代码，导致开发者难以理解生成结果、验证系统行为、追踪需求意图与建立信任。作者认为需要一种能够连接人类意图和程序实现、并支持推理的软件抽象层。

Method: 论文首先提出氛围建模的概念框架，将其定位为自然语言提示与代码之间的轻量级中间抽象；随后开展学生问卷研究，在多个AI辅助开发情境下调查参与者对LLM输出可理解性、验证工作量、信任程度，以及氛围建模实用性的感知。

Result: 摘要未报告具体的定量结果或统计显著性。研究的主要产出是通过学生感知调查收集关于LLM生成软件工件的理解、验证、信任及中间建模价值的初步证据。

Conclusion: 氛围建模被主张为提升AI辅助软件工程可信性与可解释性的潜在途径。该研究主要具有探索性，其调查结果旨在为后续验证氛围建模有效性、设计具体建模形式及开展更广泛实证研究提供依据。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts from natural language prompts. While this enables rapid prototyping and lowers the barrier to software creation, it also introduces challenges related to understanding, validation, traceability, and trust. In this paper, we argue that current AI-based development practices focus too heavily on the direct generation of code and insufficiently on intermediate representations that preserve human intent and support reasoning about system behavior. We argue for vibe modeling as a lightweight intermediate abstraction between natural language interaction and code generation. To explore its potential, we present a student survey study that examines perceptions of LLM output understanding, validation effort, trust and the perceived usefulness of vibe modeling across several AI-assisted development scenarios. Our results are intended to inform future studies for trustworthy and explainable AI-based software engineering via vibe modeling.

</details>


### [165] [Specification-Guided Synthesis of Deadlock-Free Communication Protocol Refinements with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27964)
*Yang Li,Ping Hou,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Syntropy 是一种结合多方会话类型（MPST）与大语言模型（LLM）的协议精化合成框架，能够在生成过程中施加精化约束，以提高通信协议行为正确性并避免死锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信协议中的细微行为不一致可能导致死锁，因此需要能够保持正确性与组件兼容性的安全协议替换。尽管 LLM 擅长代码生成和程序综合，但缺乏可靠保证；MPST 能提供严格的行为保证，却不擅长自动构造协议精化。

Method: Syntropy 将 MPST 规范转化为协议精化约束，并将这些约束直接融入 LLM 的生成流程，从而引导模型生成满足行为保证的协议变体。该框架在多个 LLM 上进行评估，考察有效性、语法正确性以及生成结果的多样性和复杂性。

Result: 实验表明，Syntropy 的生成结果有效率达到 95.6% 至 99.5%，同时保持较高的语法正确性，并能在多个 LLM 上生成多样且非平凡的协议精化结果。

Conclusion: 将形式化的 MPST 保证与 LLM 的生成能力结合，可以有效支持通信协议精化的自动合成。Syntropy 在可靠性、生成质量和结果多样性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Ensuring behavioural correctness in communication protocols is a central challenge in distributed software systems, as subtle inconsistencies can lead to deadlocks. In such settings, protocol refinement - the safe substitution of a protocol that preserves correctness and compatibility with other components - is essential. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and program synthesis, yet lack mechanisms to reliably produce outputs with correct behaviour. Formal specification approaches, such as multiparty session types (MPST), offer rigorous guarantees, including deadlock freedom, but provide limited support for automatically constructing protocol refinements. In this paper, we present Syntropy, a framework for synthesising protocol refinements guided by MPST specifications and LLMs. It incorporates refinement constraints directly into the generation process, ensuring the generated variants satisfy these guarantees. Our comprehensive evaluation indicates that Syntropy achieves 95.6%-99.5% validity while maintaining high syntactic correctness, and produces diverse, non-trivial refinements across multiple LLMs.

</details>


### [166] [Advancing Awkward Arrays for High-Performance CPU and GPU Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28145)
*Ianna Osborne,Manasvi Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Awkward Array面向GPU的后端改进，用于高效处理高能物理中的嵌套、变长和不规则数据，并在保持Python编程接口不变的同时提升吞吐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC分析日益依赖加速器硬件，但嵌套与变长数据难以像稠密数组一样直接映射到GPU，通常涉及间接索引、分段操作和不规则内存访问，因此需要专门的GPU执行机制。

Method: 构建基于NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries（CCCL）的CUDA实现，优化显存管理，并针对不规则数组设计分段归约算法；同时介绍后端架构、自动化验证框架，并通过CPU、CuPy和CUDA实现进行基准测试。

Result: 研究表明，新的GPU后端能够在保留现有Python编程模型的基础上提升不规则工作负载的GPU吞吐性能。摘要未给出具体性能提升比例或详细实验配置。

Conclusion: Awkward Array的GPU后端为嵌套和变长数据提供了更适合加速器的执行支持，结合CUDA库、内存优化和分段算法，有望满足HL-LHC高能物理分析对不规则数据高性能处理的需求。

Abstract: Awkward Array is a Python library for representing and processing nested, variable-length data that is widely used in high-energy physics. As HL-LHC analyses increasingly rely on accelerator hardware, efficient execution of irregular workloads has become essential. While dense numerical arrays map naturally to GPUs, nested and variable-length data structures remain significantly more difficult to accelerate because they require indirect indexing, segmented operations, and irregular memory access patterns. We present recent developments in the Awkward Array GPU backend, including CUDA implementations built on NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL), optimized memory management, and segmented reduction algorithms for ragged arrays. These developments preserve the existing Python programming model while substantially improving GPU throughput on irregular workloads. We describe the backend architecture, automated validation framework, and benchmark results comparing CPU, CuPy, and CUDA implementations.

</details>


### [167] [Integrating AI into Requirements Quality Learning in Software Engineering Education: A TPACK-Guided Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28176)
*Hansika Ekanayake Mudiyanselage,Rohan Jai Dharmaraj,Malik Abdul Sami,Zheying Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估了在硕士阶段需求工程课程中，以TPACK框架指导多智能体生成式AI工具融入“需求质量分析”作业的教学效果。结果表明，经过结构化脚手架设计后，学生主要将AI作为分析与评估辅助工具，而非自动化替代；其对部分用户故事质量维度的理解与表现得到改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正快速进入软件工程实践，但其在需求工程教育中的应用仍需兼顾学科知识、教学设计与技术能力。需求工程涉及较强的概念理解和质量判断，若缺乏教学引导，学生可能过度依赖AI输出或难以有效评估其建议。因此，研究旨在探索TPACK导向的教学设计如何支持负责任且有效的AI整合。

Method: 研究采用混合方法设计，样本量为100名学生，其中72份作业提交被纳入分析。研究将多智能体AI工具嵌入硕士课程的需求质量分析任务，并通过结构化作业要求引导学生使用AI。分析重点包括：学生实际使用AI的方式、学生对用户故事质量标准的理解变化，以及其对AI优势、局限与可用性的感知。

Result: 学生总体上选择性使用AI，主要用于辅助分析、比较和评价需求质量，而不是直接让AI完成任务。对于较具体、结构化的质量维度，AI支持带来的对齐改进较明显，尤其体现在价值表达和可测试性方面；可协商性维度的效果则不一致。学生表现出有条件的信任，即会审查、修订和补充AI输出，而非完全接受。学生也报告称AI有助于提升其对质量标准的意识，但工具可用性仍存在中等程度的挑战。

Conclusion: TPACK导向的脚手架能够较好地将AI工具能力与需求工程课程目标、学科内容相匹配。有效的AI融入不应以自动化取代学习者判断为目标，而应通过明确任务结构、质量标准和反思机制，促进学生将AI作为分析与评价的认知支持工具。研究为需求工程教育中负责任地设计和部署生成式AI学习活动提供了实践依据。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) in software engineering (SE) practice creates a need for pedagogically grounded approaches to AI integration in SE education, especially in conceptually intensive subjects such as requirements engineering (RE). This study examines a TPACK-guided integration of a multi-agent AI tool into a master-level RE assignment on requirements quality analysis. Using a mixed-methods design (N=100; 72 submissions analysed), we examine how structured assignment design shaped students' AI use, affected their understanding of user story quality criteria, and influenced their perceptions of AI's benefits and limitations. Results show that students used the AI tool selectively, mainly as support for analysis and evaluation rather than automation. Alignment improvements were most evident for structurally concrete requirements quality dimensions, such as value articulation and testability, while negotiability showed mixed effects. Students reported conditional trust, active refinement, and increased awareness of quality criteria, alongside moderate usability challenges. The findings show that TPACK-guided scaffolding can align AI affordances with pedagogical goals and RE content, offering design guidance for responsible AI integration in RE education.

</details>


### [168] [Agentic Metaverse Services: A New As-a-Service Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28242)
*Xiaofei Xu,Quan Z. Sheng,Zhongjie Wang,Boualem Benatallah,Xiao Wang,Ruipeng Han*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“智能体化元宇宙服务”（AMServ）与“元宇宙环境下的智能体即服务”（Meta-AaaS）概念，旨在将生成式人工智能驱动的智能体能力封装为可定制服务，用于元宇宙中的业务处理与服务交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能推动智能体由传统对话系统向具备自主学习、多模态交互、决策、执行、协作和内容生成能力的智能体化系统演进。现有元宇宙服务需要更强的自动化、个性化与协同能力，因此有必要构建面向元宇宙场景的新型智能体服务范式。

Method: 论文采用综述与概念框架分析方法：首先梳理生成式人工智能赋能下智能体和服务的演进特征；随后阐释智能体服务在元宇宙环境中的角色与运行原则；进一步定义AMServ及作为其实现路径的Meta-AaaS，归纳其形态、特征和基本原理；最后结合典型应用案例讨论未来发展趋势与研究方向。

Result: 论文形成了以AMServ为核心、以Meta-AaaS为实现范式的概念体系。该体系强调将感知、决策、执行、协作及内容生成等能力模块化、服务化和可组合化，以支持面向用户需求的定制智能体服务，并适配元宇宙中的业务流程处理。摘要未提供实验数据、性能评测或具体系统实现结果。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要贡献是提出并系统化阐述生成式人工智能时代元宇宙智能体服务的新范式。AMServ和Meta-AaaS有望促进元宇宙服务从静态数字功能向自主、协同、个性化服务演进；后续研究可重点关注服务编排、智能体协作、可信治理、互操作性、评测标准及实际应用部署。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is reconstructing the digital virtual world, upgrading agents through enhancing their abilities in autonomous learning, multi-modal interaction, content generation, and collaborative decision-making. In particular, the shift from conversational chatbots to agentic AI, the most recent significant technical breakthrough of GenAI, has brought a new form of services, agentic services and Agent-as-a-Service (AaaS), in which the agent's abilities are encapsulated, such as perception, decision-making, execution, collaboration, and content generation, to provide the customized agent services to users. The metaverse is a virtual ecosystem for human life, work, creation, and entertainment, supported by the new generation of digital technologies. Through combining agentic services and the metaverse, an Agentic Metaverse Service, denoted as AMServ, is produced for metaverse business processing, as a new form of metaverse service. The AaaS in the metaverse environment, denoted as Meta-AaaS, as an approach to realize AMServ, has become a new paradigm of agentic services and service computing. This paper overviews the evolution and new features of agents and services empowered by GenAI, reveals the roles and principles of agentic services in the metaverse environment, presents the forms, characteristics, and principles of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, discusses the typical application examples of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, and finally points out the new tendencies and research directions of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS. The AMServ and the Meta-AaaS will bring great opportunities to human society and services in the AI era, and promote the rapid development of emerging service industries in the future.

</details>


### [169] [Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28271)
*Andras Ferenczi,Jordan Docherty,Mariya Bessonov,Matthew Findlay,Krishna Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“Locksmith Loop”智能体测试合成方法，用于验证COBOL遗留程序迁移至Java后的功能等价性，并在多个案例中显著提升分支覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL到Java迁移需要大量测试，但通常缺乏有效测试数据，且难以覆盖边界条件和深层分支。仅依赖常规输入搜索容易陷入覆盖率平台期，因此需要一种能够主动识别并突破路径阻塞条件的方法。

Method: 方法先在通用硬件上分别运行经Mock插桩的COBOL源程序与生成的Java程序。随后，智能体循环执行针对输入Mock的Witness Search以探索分支，并进行保持两端行为一致性的变异。当探索到达路由边界时，分析器识别阻碍进一步执行的“Locked Paragraph”，据此生成新的测试与变异策略。每个候选测试均通过确定性的COBOL-Java结果比对进行验收。

Result: 在3个COBOL-Java迁移案例中，该方法均突破了纯输入搜索的覆盖率瓶颈。两个开源程序达到接近完整的覆盖率；一个规模为4,114行的内部生产级COBOL程序达到91.90%的分支覆盖率。所有被接受的测试案例中，生成Java与COBOL参考实现的确定性结果一致。

Conclusion: Locksmith Loop将智能体驱动的路径探索、锁定条件诊断、等价性保持变异与确定性差分预言机结合，可有效提升遗留程序迁移验证的测试覆盖与可信度。其主要价值在于为智能体生成代码提供可重复、可执行的功能等价性验证机制。

Abstract: Migration of legacy COBOL programs to Java requires extensive testing to ensure correct functionality. This effort is often complicated by the lack of test data and the difficulty of validating all corner cases. In this paper we propose a novel agentic test-synthesis method, the "Locksmith Loop," which is initiated by preparing two runtime environments: the COBOL source and the generated Java target are each instrumented with mocks and executed off-mainframe on commodity hardware, then an iterative agentic loop performs Witness Search over input mocks to penetrate program branches, followed by parity-preserving mutations. When routing boundaries are reached, an analyzer identifies a Locked Paragraph: a condition preventing deeper exploration. Across three COBOL-Java case studies, spanning two open-source programs and one internal production-like COBOL program and ranging from 430 to 4,114 source lines, Locksmith consistently improved coverage beyond input-search plateaus, reaching nearly complete coverage on the two open-source programs and 91.90% branch coverage on the internal production-like COBOL program. The generated Java matched the COBOL reference under deterministic parity checks in all accepted test cases. Through these findings we demonstrate, to the best of our knowledge, a novel approach for validating agentic coding output using a deterministic oracle.

</details>


### [170] [From Textual Requirements to Microservice Architectures - A Comprehensive Evaluation of LLM-Based Design Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28307)
*Danyllo Albuquerque,José Renan,Guillermo Rodríguez,Guillermo Rodríguez,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França,Mirko Perkusich,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估OpenAI o3能否仅依据自然语言需求生成微服务架构。结果显示，少样本提示（FS）显著优于零样本提示（ZS）：服务识别F1从0.79提升至0.97，服务通信恢复F1从0.61提升至0.82；专家也认为FS结果在模块化、连贯性和合理性方面更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务拆分通常依赖代码分析，难以支持只有文本需求的早期设计阶段。该研究旨在检验大语言模型是否能够连接需求工程与架构设计，直接从需求中推导服务边界及服务间交互，并提供相应的实证证据。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，在Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统上，使用OpenAI o3分别进行零样本和少样本提示，每个系统与条件仅执行一次。定量评估将生成架构与参考架构对比，以精确率、召回率和F1衡量服务识别及通信关系恢复；定性评估采用盲审专家评价正确性、完整性、模块化和合理性，并综合开放式反馈。

Result: 少样本提示下的服务识别与参考架构高度一致，F1为0.97，高于零样本的0.79。通信恢复更困难：零样本结果呈现较密集的依赖图，虽然召回率较高但精确率较低，F1为0.61；少样本提示减少了缺乏依据的依赖关系，将通信恢复F1提升至0.82。专家评审与量化结果一致，认为FS生成的架构更具模块化、一致性和工程合理性。

Conclusion: OpenAI o3在示例驱动的提示条件下，具备从自然语言需求生成微服务架构的潜力，尤其适合辅助早期架构设计。少样本示例是提升服务边界识别和交互关系可靠性的关键因素。但研究仅涵盖两个较小系统、单次运行及单一模型，结论具有明显的模型与任务上下文依赖性，尚不能证明该能力对不同模型、系统规模或需求类型具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: Microservice architectures have become dominant for modernizing monolithic systems, yet identifying appropriate services remains challenging and largely manual. Existing decomposition approaches are predominantly code-centric, limiting applicability in early design stages where only textual requirements are available.
