Despite advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), limited empirical evidence exists on their ability to synthesize complete microservice architectures from natural-language requirements, including service definitions and inter-service interactions. This study investigates whether an LLM can bridge requirements engineering and architectural design, generating architectures solely from textual requirements and evaluating structural agreement and perceived quality of results. We conduct a mixed-method study using OpenAI o3 under zero-shot (ZS) and few-shot (FS) prompting across two systems (Bookstore, PetClinic), one execution per system/condition. Architectures are evaluated through (i) comparison with reference architectures using precision, recall, and F1-score for service identification and communication recovery, and (ii) a blinded expert assessment of correctness, completeness, modularity, and plausibility, plus open feedback synthesis. OpenAI o3 identifies services with higher agreement under FS prompting (F1 = 0.79 for ZS versus = 0.97 for FS). Communication recovery is more challenging: ZS produces dense architectures with high recall but low precision (F1 = 0.61), while FS improves agreement, reaching F1 = 0.82 and reducing unsupported dependencies. Expert evaluation corroborates these results, with FS architectures perceived as more modular, coherent, and plausible than ZS outputs. OpenAI o3 shows potential for requirements-driven synthesis when guided by exemplar prompting. Results are model- and context-specific from two small systems, not model-independent proof.

</details>


### [171] [Structural Validation of LLM-Generated Microservice Decompositions Using Source-Code Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28331)
*Daniel Silva,Renan Alves,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了大语言模型生成微服务拆分方案对源代码结构依赖的保留程度，并指出必须控制类到服务映射的覆盖率，否则不同提示策略的比较可能存在方法偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体系统向微服务迁移是软件现代化的重要任务。尽管大语言模型能够依据文本需求生成语义上合理的拆分方案，但这些方案是否保留了代码实际实现的结构依赖尚不明确。

Method: 研究以PetClinic和Bookstore为对象，使用OpenAI o3生成零样本与少样本提示下的微服务拆分方案；提出基于静态依赖分析的自动验证流水线，并以依赖保留率TPD和依赖违反率TVD衡量结构一致性。随后通过稳健性分析，对类到服务映射的覆盖差异进行归一化控制。

Result: 在未充分控制映射覆盖率时，不同提示策略可能表现出表面上的结构差异。归一化后，零样本和少样本提示的结构遵从性相当：PetClinic的TPD为68.0%，Bookstore的TPD为83.3%。

Conclusion: 评估大语言模型生成的微服务架构时，不能仅比较提示策略下的原始依赖指标，必须显式控制类到服务映射覆盖率。否则，观测到的性能差异可能源于评估方法偏差，而非拆分方案本身的架构质量差异。

Abstract: Decomposing monolithic systems into microservices is a key activity in software modernization. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate semantically plausible decompositions from textual requirements, it remains unclear whether these proposals preserve the structural dependencies implemented in the source code. This paper evaluates the structural adherence of microservice decompositions generated by OpenAI o3 for the PetClinic and Bookstore systems. We propose an automated validation pipeline based on static dependency analysis and compare zero-shot and few-shot prompting using dependency preservation (TPD) and dependency violation (TVD) metrics. A robustness analysis was conducted to control for differences in class-to-service mapping coverage. After normalization, both prompting strategies produced equivalent structural adherence, achieving TPD values of 68.0% (PetClinic) and 83.3% (Bookstore). The findings demonstrate that structural evaluations of LLM-generated decompositions should explicitly control for mapping coverage, as apparent differences between prompting strategies may otherwise reflect methodological bias rather than genuine architectural quality.

</details>


### [172] [Demystifying Solana Bots: From GitHub Blueprints to On-Chain Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28424)
*Xiaoye Zheng,Yujing Chen,Minghao Wu,David Lo,Difan Xie,Daoyuan Wu,Xiaohu Yang,Zhiyuan Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对Solana机器人开展大规模实证研究，结合GitHub代码仓库与链上交易数据，构建机器人类型学、提炼通用运行流程，并分析不同机器人在交易平台和资产上的链上行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana具有高吞吐量和低交易成本，降低了大规模提交交易的门槛，使自动化机器人广泛参与链上交易。现有研究虽覆盖系统性能、合约安全及部分链上现象，但缺乏对机器人实际实现机制及其可观测链上执行特征的系统性理解。

Method: 研究从两个互补视角收集数据：第一，采集586个GitHub机器人代码仓库，分析其功能模块、实现模式和运行逻辑；第二，选取200个Solana机器人地址，分析超过4400万笔链上交易。基于代码实现归纳功能分类与操作流程，再将这些实现特征与链上交易行为、交易平台及资产活动进行关联比较。

Result: 论文提出由15类机器人组成的分类体系，并将其归入五个功能领域，包括交易操作、最大可提取价值相关操作及链上分析等。研究发现，多数机器人实现中存在共享的五阶段操作流水线；同时，不同类别机器人在交易频率、平台选择、资产交互和执行模式等链上交易行为上呈现系统性差异。

Conclusion: Solana机器人并非单一类型的自动化交易程序，而是具有多样化目标、模块化实现和可辨识链上指纹的软件生态。代码层面的实现分析与链上行为分析相结合，能够更可靠地理解机器人活动，并可为后续检测、测量、设计和运维自动化链上程序提供依据。

Abstract: Solana is an emerging blockchain platform designed for high throughput and low transaction fees, making it inexpensive to submit transactions at scale and, consequently, increasing exposure to bot spamming and related financial exploitation. Solana bots are typically off-chain software systems that operate in a competitive on-chain execution environment by constructing and submitting transactions, and the bot-related transactions on the decentralized exchanges exceed 250 million dollars in daily trading volume in January 2026. Prior studies on Solana have examined system performance, smart-contract security, and specific on-chain phenomena. However, we still lack a systematic understanding of what Solana bots implement in practice and how these implementations manifest as observable on-chain execution fingerprints. To address this gap, we performed a large-scale empirical study of Solana bots from two complementary views: (i) 586 bot repositories collected from GitHub, and (ii) 200 bot addresses on Solana, with over 44 million on-chain transactions. Our study derives an implementation-grounded taxonomy of Solana bots comprising 15 categories grouped into five domains (e.g., Trading Operations, MEV, and On-chain Analytics), identifies a largely shared five-stage operational pipeline manifested in bot implementations, and uncovers systematic variation in on-chain trading behaviors of Solana bots across diverse trading platforms and assets. Based on our findings, we highlight future research directions, and provide recommendations for building and operating bots on the Solana blockchain.

</details>


### [173] [Emerging Challenges in Threat Modeling for GenAI-Augmented Systems: A View from the Trenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28431)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Manish Mahesh Kumar,Nohemí Villarreal,Pankaj Pantel,Immo Brueggemann,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估了面向生成式人工智能系统的威胁建模方法在中小企业场景中的适用性，发现现有方法识别的威胁存在明显差异，且对供应链与人因安全等生成式人工智能特有风险支持不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统威胁建模方法如STRIDE主要面向常规软件架构，难以充分覆盖生成式人工智能系统带来的新型风险。因此，研究旨在筛选并验证适合生成式人工智能增强型系统的威胁建模技术，并考察其在中小企业开发流程中的落地可行性。

Method: 研究采用快速文献综述筛选相关威胁建模技术，选出三种候选方法；随后将其系统应用于一个工业界生成式人工智能增强系统案例。研究同时比较各方法识别的威胁类型，并收集从业者对方法易用性、实施工作量、流程集成及采纳障碍的反馈。

Result: 三种方法识别出的威胁范围和重点不同，表明单一方法难以全面覆盖生成式人工智能风险。尤其在软件供应链风险和以人为中心的安全问题方面，现有方法提供的支持较有限。实践人员还指出，这些方法在中小企业环境中的使用会受到额外工作量、开发流程适配和组织采纳成本等因素影响。

Conclusion: 生成式人工智能系统需要比传统方法更具针对性的威胁建模框架。未来方法应增强对模型、数据、依赖组件供应链及用户交互相关风险的覆盖，并兼顾中小企业资源有限、流程轻量化和易集成的实际需求。

Abstract: Threat modeling remains a central task in secure software engineering, as it enables the identification of security issues from system architectures. As Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) becomes increasingly pervasive across software systems, traditional threat modeling methods (e.g., STRIDE) are insufficient to assess emerging GenAI-specific risks. In this work, we present the first results from an exploratory assessment of GenAI-aware threat modeling methods in a Small and Medium Enterprise (SME) setting. For this, we conducted a rapid literature review to select relevant techniques and systematically applied three shortlisted methods to an industrial case study involving a GenAI-augmented system. The results highlight differences in the threats identified by each technique and reveal limited support for certain GenAI-specific risk categories, particularly those related to software supply chains and human-centered security issues. We further report practitioners' perceptions of the usability and integration of these methods in SME development workflows, including their perceived effort and adoption challenges.

</details>


### [174] [CoGate: Confidence-Gated Co-Decoding for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28529)
*Minghao Hu,Lannan Luo,Allen Roush,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CoGate，一种基于置信度门控的协同解码方法，用于在代码生成中动态调节安全专家模型对主模型输出的影响，从而提升生成代码的安全性与功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同解码方法在每一步都依据安全专家模型进行引导，但未考虑专家模型自身的置信度。当输入属于未见模式或分布外上下文时，安全专家可能做出不可靠判断，进而误导代码生成过程。

Method: CoGate在协同解码框架中引入置信度门控机制：根据安全专家模型对当前预测的置信度，动态控制其对目标代码模型解码分布的干预强度。作者在CodeGen、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen-Coder和StarCoder等后端模型上实现并测试该方法。

Result: 在HumanEval、安全测试套件和CWEval等基准上，CoGate整体优于既有协同解码方法CoSec+。其中，在CWEval上，Func-Sec@10指标最高提升12.6%。

Conclusion: 通过将专家模型置信度纳入解码控制，CoGate能够减少低置信度安全指导带来的负面影响，在维持代码功能表现的同时改善安全性，表明置信度感知的协同解码是更稳健的安全代码生成策略。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used for code generation, but they can also produce insecure programs due to patterns learned from their pretraining data. Decoding-time steering has become an important solution to this problem: a small expert model is combined with the target model at each step to generate more secure code, which is referred to as co-decoding. However, the acceptance rule for existing co-decoding approaches does not consider the expert model's confidence. When the security expert is unconfident due to unseen patterns or out-of-distribution (OOD) contexts, its guidance can therefore be misleading. To address the challenge, we propose CoGate, a confidence-gated co-decoding approach that controls the expert's influence on the co-decoding process based on its confidence. We implement our approach and evaluate it across multiple LLM backends (CodeGen, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen-Coder, StarCoder) on several code generation benchmarks (HumanEval, security suite, and CWEval). Our approach outperforms existing co-decoding methods (CoSec+) across multiple benchmarks, achieving up to a 12.6% gain of Func-Sec@10 on CWEval.

</details>


### [175] [PAIChecker: Uncovering and Checking PR-Issue Misalignment in SWE-Bench-Like Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28587)
*Manyi Wang,Junjielong Xu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出SWE-bench类基准中PR与Issue的自动配对常存在语义或实现层面的错位，并提出多智能体系统PAIChecker进行检测与验证，在多个数据集和模型骨干上取得较高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE-bench类基准通常依据PR描述中的Issue引用构建“问题—补丁”样本，但大型软件仓库的开发维护流程复杂，使引用关系不一定表示该PR确实解决了对应Issue。此类错配会损害评测样本的有效性，并可能导致对大语言模型缺陷修复能力的错误估计。

Method: 作者系统审查SWE-bench Verified样本，归纳出5类错配模式和11种细粒度场景。随后提出PAIChecker，其三阶段流程包括：识别特定错配模式；汇总多个智能体的判定标签；通过代码级验证逐步确认PR补丁是否真正对应Issue需求。该设计结合语义判断与实现验证，以增强检测准确性、泛化能力和结果可靠性。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified中，13.6%的实例被发现存在PR—Issue错配。PAIChecker在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上的实验中，相比基线在四种LLM骨干下均表现最佳，二分类准确率最高分别达到92.12%和91.67%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，基于PR引用自动构造的软件工程评测基准存在不可忽略的标签与任务定义噪声。PAIChecker通过多智能体协同及代码级验证，可为未来SWE-bench类基准的数据清洗、质量审计和可扩展构建提供有效方案。

Abstract: SWE-bench-like benchmarks are widely used for evaluating LLM's issue resolution capability. They typically follow a common construction pipeline: each PR (Pull Request) is paired with its linked issue by extracting issue references from the PR description; the issue description is used as the problem statement, and the PR patch serves as the test oracle. However, due to the inherent complexity of developing and maintaining large repositories, such PR-Issue pairings are often misaligned in practice. In this work, we systematically study SWE-bench Verified instances, finding that 13.6% exhibit misalignment across five patterns in eleven fine-grained scenarios. To enable reliable and scalable construction of those benchmarks in the future, we propose PAIChecker, a multi-agent system for checking PR-Issue misalignment in SWE-bench-like benchmarks. Specifically, PAIChecker adopts a three-phase design that combines specific pattern identification, cross-agent label synthesis, and code-level validation, thereby enabling more accurate, generalizable, and progressively verified detection. Experiments on SWE-Gym and SWE-bench Multilingual show that PAIchecker achieves the best performance across all four LLM backbones, reaching up to 92.12% and 91.67% binary accuracy, respectively.

</details>


### [176] [Change2Task: From Repository Changes to Executable Coding Agent Tasks and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28591)
*Haomin Qi,Xingliang Wang,Xuanqi Gao,Baihui Sang,Xin Zhang,Minghua Ma,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Change2Task利用代码仓库历史记录，将已合并的拉取请求转换为可执行、可验证的编码代理任务，并在现代健康代码版本上重建任务环境。系统覆盖五类任务，在1,130个候选变更上实现79.6%的验证构造成功率，比基于拉取请求的基线多恢复29.2%的验证任务，同时降低10.8%的全流程成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理的训练、基准测试和持续评估需要持续、可靠且可执行的数据。现有任务构造通常依赖重复的环境配置、人工整理和过时的软件状态，导致构造成本高、验证困难，并难以适应代码仓库的持续演化。

Method: 系统以仓库历史中的已合并拉取请求为开发证据，将其对齐到同一仓库的现代健康版本。针对历史变更，采用补丁逆转（Patch Reversal）、代码映射（Code Mapping）或代理重构（Agent Reconstruction）恢复任务状态，并验证从健康基础状态到任务状态再到恢复状态的完整生命周期。评估覆盖缺陷修复、功能添加、测试生成、应用程序接口迁移和安全修复五类编码代理任务。

Result: 在1,130个符合构造条件的源变更中，Change2Task的验证任务构造成功率为79.6%。在匹配候选集上，相比基于拉取请求的构造基线，验证任务恢复量提升29.2%。历史重建和代理重构案例在代理评估中的匹配结果一致率最高达到98.0%；复用现代基础版本使完整流程的测量成本降低10.8%。

Conclusion: Change2Task能够将开发者真实历史证据规模化转化为可执行且经过生命周期验证的编码代理数据，并有效提升任务产出、结果可靠性和成本效率。该方法适合支持编码代理的训练、基准测试与持续评估，但其效果仍依赖仓库健康状态、历史变更可映射性及任务验证质量。

Abstract: Scaling coding agents requires a continuing supply of executable data for training, benchmarking, and continuous evaluation. Each task must couple a realistic software state with a specification, development tools, and reliable verification. To expand this supply, we present Change2Task, a system grounded in repository history that converts merged pull requests into verified tasks on healthy modern revisions of the same repository. It aligns historical evidence with evolved code, reconstructs task states through Patch Reversal, Code Mapping, or Agent Reconstruction, and validates the lifecycle from a healthy base to a task state and a restored state. By deriving multiple tasks grounded in developer evidence from maintained environments, Change2Task provides executable data for coding agent training and evaluation while reducing repeated environment setup, storage, and task construction effort. We evaluate the system through five common and widely adopted coding agent task families: Bug Fix, Feature Addition, Test Generation, Application Programming Interface Migration, and Security Repair. Starting from 1,130 source changes eligible for construction, Change2Task achieves 79.6% verified task construction success across these task families. On a matched candidate set, it recovers 29.2% more verified tasks than a construction baseline based on pull requests. Historical and reconstructed cases achieve up to 98.0% matched outcome agreement under agent evaluation, while reuse of modern bases reduces measured expenditure across the complete pipeline by 10.8%.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [BMOA: Baseline-Mechanism-Outcome Attribution for Compiler-Induced Numerical Deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27270)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Chunwei Xia,Mengfei Ren,Jianfeng Zhu,Qiang Guan*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出BMOA（Baseline--Mechanism--Outcome Attribution）框架，用于将浮点数差异从简单的“匹配/不匹配”判断，细分为比较基线、编译器机制证据和数值精度结果三类可审计归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值测试流程常将观测到的浮点差异直接视为错误，却未区分差异相对于何种基线产生、是否能由特定编译器变换解释，以及该差异是否实际降低了相对于高精度参考的准确性。因此，需要一种能够保留证据不足、混合或模糊结论的诊断方法。

Method: BMOA将每项观察记录拆分为：比较关系与系统边界的定义、由证据支持的编译器行为机制、以及相对于参考结果评估的精度后果。框架联合使用严格浮点语义比较、局部变换分析、可复现性测试、跨编译器比较和高精度参考比较；每条记录保存输入、编译配置、数值指标及支持性工件，以支持复核。实验覆盖六个科学计算内核、确定性压力输入族，以及ARM64上的受控Clang配置。

Result: 评估构建了包含1,276条记录的归因语料库和162个实例的受控机制矩阵。结果表明，不同基线会显著改变诊断结论；由编译器引起的结果偏离并不必然意味着数值精度下降；在目标测试矩阵中，消去误差和大动态范围输入最容易暴露显著影响。

Conclusion: BMOA将原始浮点不匹配转化为明确、可审计且受证据边界约束的归因记录。该框架本身不是形式化证明系统，但可为面向编译器感知数值正确性的未来形式化规范、证明义务和验证方法提供经验基础。

Abstract: Formalizing compiler-aware numerical correctness requires distinguishing what an observed floating-point difference means, what compiler behavior the evidence supports, and what numerical consequence follows. Existing testing workflows often collapse these questions into a pass/fail mismatch. We introduce Baseline--Mechanism--Outcome Attribution (BMOA), a diagnostic framework that separates the comparison relation and system boundary, the evidence-supported compiler mechanism, and the reference-qualified accuracy outcome. BMOA combines operational strict floating-point, transformation-local, reproducibility, cross-compiler, and higher-precision comparisons, while preserving mixed, ambiguous, and unknown attributions when evidence is insufficient. Each record retains inputs, configurations, numerical metrics, and supporting artifacts for audit. We evaluate BMOA on six scientific-computing kernels, deterministic stress-input families, and controlled Clang configurations on ARM64. A 1,276-record attribution corpus and a 162-instance controlled mechanism matrix show that baseline choice changes diagnoses, compiler-induced deviation does not imply accuracy loss, and cancellation and large dynamic range expose the strongest effects within the targeted matrix. BMOA converts raw mismatches into explicit, auditable, evidence-bounded records. Although it is not itself a proof system, these records provide an empirical foundation for future formal specifications and proof obligations for compiler-aware numerical correctness.

</details>


### [178] [Tempo: Reconstructing Synchronous Reactive Programming with OCaml 5 Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23550)
*Frédéric Dabrowski*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Tempo：一个基于 OCaml 5 代数效应与深层处理器的库级同步响应式运行时，用于在普通 OCaml 中重构 ReactiveML 的核心执行机制，并与 ReactiveML 比较性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用语言扩展能够提供确定性的逻辑时刻、广播信号、协作线程与动态进程等同步响应式语义，但会增加语言和工具链成本。作者希望验证这些机制能否仅依赖标准 OCaml 5 的语言特性实现，同时评估由此带来的性能代价。

Method: Tempo 将响应式挂起点表示为效应操作，并由深层效应处理器捕获和具体化延续，将其封装为任务。运行时依据逻辑时刻语义调度这些任务，以实现协作执行、信号通信和动态进程行为。论文进一步通过与 ReactiveML 的对比实验，测量库实现相对于专用编译器支持的额外开销，并分析主要成本来源。

Result: 摘要表明，Tempo 能够在普通 OCaml 5 中复现该同步响应式模型的核心机制。性能比较用于量化库级重构的运行时开销，并定位主导成本的运行时机制；但摘要未提供具体的性能数据、基准程序或成本占比。

Conclusion: OCaml 5 的代数效应和深层处理器足以支持以库形式实现具有逻辑时刻语义的同步响应式运行时。该方案提升了在现有 OCaml 生态中采用响应式编程模型的可行性，但相较于专用语言实现会存在可测量的运行时成本，后续优化应聚焦于实验识别出的关键运行时机制。

Abstract: Synchronous reactive programming gives reactive systems a deterministic temporal structure by organizing execution into logical instants and signal-based communication. Boussinot's synchronous reactive model extends this setting with cooperative threads, broadcast signals, and dynamic processes; ReactiveML brings that model into a strict, typed, higher-order functional language. This paper studies whether the same core mechanisms can be reconstructed inside ordinary OCaml 5, rather than exposed by a dedicated language extension. We present Tempo, a library runtime based on algebraic effects and deep handlers: effect operations delimit reactive suspension points, and the handler reifies captured continuations as tasks scheduled by logical-instant semantics. A comparative study with ReactiveML quantifies the overhead of this library-level reconstruction and identifies the runtime mechanisms that dominate its cost.

</details>
